2026-10-23 Scene Master (« Matrice de Scène » MAT-08, real audio): a Scene's level is `scene.gain_db` (`kernel/scene_schema.js#validateSceneGainDb`, `sceneGainDb`, `sceneGainLinear`; set by `scene.update { gain_db }`, 0 dB stored as nothing, -120..+12). Playback: `runtime_transport.js#buildTransportTimeline` projects `sceneGains` [{ sceneId, start, end, gain }] (section-relative for a section playback) and marks content tracks `followsScenes` (the metronome does not); `molecule_support.js` keeps them (`normalizeSceneGains`, `sceneGainAt`, `collectActiveClips` → `trackFollowsScenes`). `molecule_render_schedule.js#scheduleWindow` cuts every voice at the Scene edges inside it (`scenePieces`, `pieceShape`: pieces on their exact frames, 5 ms linear cross-fade through the engine's attack/release, voice ids `…:s<n>`), so the linear Timeline, loop passes and cells all play each Scene at its level; clock-less engines apply `_sceneGain` in the envelope sync. Live: new mixin `eVe/core/media_engine/molecule_session_scenes.js` (`setSceneGain` over `session.sceneVoices`: waiting pieces rescheduled on their frame, sounding ones `setVoiceGain`), `runtime_transport.js`/`runtime_cells.js#setSceneGain`, API `setGroupTimelineSceneGain({ group_id, scene_id, gain_db })` (`runtime_api_transport.js`, no history). UI: new `eVe/domains/rendering/molecule_mix_scene_master.js` (`createSceneMaster`: first strip of the Mixer unfolded in the Scene view, canonical `buildBevyPanelFaderNode`, value, Scene name in the band; drag heard live, one `scene.update` on release, double-click types it), wired in `project_view_scene_view_runtime.js`. Probes `temp/scene_master/schedule.probe.mjs` 7/7, `temp/scene_master/audio_real_app.probe.mjs` (Web Kira output). Search keywords: master de scène, niveau de scène, gain de scène, fondu croisé, découpe de voix.

People directory: new owner `eVe/intuition/tools/contact_public_directory.js` (`readPublicDirectoryOf(owner)` one read at a time, last answer kept; `knownPublicDirectory`); `contact_model.js#collectPeopleDirectory({ local })`, `findPersonById`, `readPublicDirectory({ local })`; `dashboard_contact_items.js#listContactItems(..., { local })`. Media list: `audio_core_media.js#list_user_media_files` reads the server list and the device's takes together (wave per source, `local: true` skips the Web server, server read bounded 10 s, no `auth_required` on Web without cloud token); `media.js#listUserMedia({ local })`. Non-blocking network reads: Home panel `contacts.getPreferences`, Privacy rules, Sommeil page circle posts, Communication sync (after the local selection), conversation call lines first, weather fetch bounded 10 s. Tool catalogue refresh (`intuition/runtime/tool.js#refreshToolCatalogFromDatabase`, on window focus = first touch on iOS) goes by slices of 6 and skips unchanged entries (`tool_state.js` `TOOL_CANONICAL_PERSISTED`, `TOOL_CATALOG_RECORD_VERSIONS`). Engines: internal EPUB `epub_document.js` + `zip_archive_reader.js` (DecompressionStream), pagination `book_pagination.js` (`createTextEditingLayout`, anchors survive reflow), PDF `pdf_document.js` (vendored pdf.js `atome/src/assets/vendor/pdfjs`, `scripts/bundle-pdfjs.js`, RGBA → `bevyTexture`). Session `book_reader_session.js` (layout, page turns, debounced position), audio `audiobook_player.js` (`PlayRecordCore` directly — `loadAsset`/`playVoice`/`stopVoice`/`releaseTransientAsset`, one chapter loaded). UI: panel surface `book_reader` (`bevy_panel_book_reader_runtime.js` + `_view.js`) hosted on the stage by `dashboard_mystic_stage.js#openPanel({ progress })` (the band's bar for a hosted panel). Rich text gained `italic` (`rich_text_style.js`, `text_editing_layout.js`, `bevy_media_texture_resolver.js`). Offline manifest also precaches `.pfb`/`.bcmap` (`server/offline_app_assets.js`). Range chunks travel as `base64` (never `data`: the WS client takes a reply's `data` as its envelope; `server/public_source_fetch.js`, `eVe/domains/media/public_source_client.js`). `dashboard_surface_tools.js#paint` is serialized (a late mount no longer unmounts the newer band). i18n `eVe/i18n/languages_books.js`. Probes `temp/books/`. Search keywords: livre, livre audio, book, ebook, audiobook, epub, pdf, gutenberg, librivox, lecteur, reader.

2026-10-16 Legacy wallpaper repair: `eVe/intuition/tools/background_legacy_media.js` (composed into the Background tool controller by `eVe/intuition/tools/background.js`, exported there as `migrateLegacyBackgroundMedia`) completes a wallpaper preference saved before media atomes (`backgroundImageUrl` naming a `/file/` or `/api/uploads/` file, empty `backgroundImageAtomeId`, current choice or saved preset). Only the device holding the file acts — native: `ensureMediaLocallyAvailable`; Web: the file is in this browser's vault (`guest_workspace_store.js#listGuestFiles`) — then `asset_box_atome_store.js#resolveUploadAtomeId(file, { scope: 'global' })` and the existing `schedulePreferenceSave({ immediate: true })`. `eVe/user/background.js` only requests it (`requestLegacyBackgroundRepair`) when it applies such a preference; one verdict per file and owner per session. No new registry or API surface. 2026-10-17: `media_api_local_availability.js#ensureMediaLocallyAvailable` returns `userId` (the folder the file was found in) and names it in `localUrl` (`?media_user_id=`); native readers (wallpaper, assistant attachments, Molecule, media hydration) send that `X-User-Id`.

2026-10-16 Wallpaper across devices: `eVe/intuition/tools/background_image.js` uploads a picked/downloaded wallpaper with its account-wide media atome (`sendFileToServer(..., { scope: 'global' })`) and always stores `preferences.background.backgroundImageAtomeId`; a Media-library pick listed without id goes through `eVe/domains/media/asset_box_atome_store.js#resolveUploadAtomeId` (listed id, else atome whose path names the file, else created). `eVe/user/background.js#resolveProtectedBackgroundObjectUrl(url, mediaAtomeId)` reads such a wallpaper from the atome on the current device (native: `ensureMediaLocallyAvailable` + local auth headers; browser: `/api/uploads/<file>?media_user_id=`), never from the picking device's stored path; `user_surface_background_texture_runtime.js` keys the signature on `atome:<id>` and retries on sync delivery events while the atome is not yet local. No new registry or API surface.

Only layouts with a centre disc (`atomDisc`) turn; `dashboard_mystic` stays portrait. Probes `temp/dashboard_responsive/{shape,viewports}.probe.mjs`.

2026-10-11 Blur pyramid without storage textures: `atome/renderers/bevy-core/src/workspace_blur_pyramid.rs` (installed by `workspace_blur.rs`) renders mip levels 1..N with one colour-attachment pass per level (`assets/shaders/backdrop_downsample.wgsl`, integer 2x2 average like Bevy's SPD). It replaces Bevy 0.19 `mip_generation`, whose six storage textures per pass exceed the A12Z iPad WebGPU limit of four and made Bevy's default `RenderErrorHandler` quit the event loop (uniform grey canvas). The pyramid image no longer carries `STORAGE_BINDING`; both former mip call sites (capture after `upscaling`, ordered snapshot) use the chain. `platforms/web/bevy-renderer/src/render_error.rs` keeps Bevy's quit policy but exposes the first wgpu error as `render_error` in `read_atome_bevy_web_diagnostics()`; `bevy_web_presentation_runtime.js` rejects startup with `bevy_renderer_render_error:`. No new canvas, renderer, public API or durable state. See `known-bug-solutions/ipad-a12-grey-canvas-storage-limit/`.

The two existing start methods reject delayed starts after release. Regression coverage remains in tool_runtime_home_lazy_load and dashboard_basic_runtime suites.

2026-10-09 Panels with large data: `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_accordion_list.js#buildAccordionList` (the shared list of Atome of Contacts and the Communication inbox, where News land) takes an optional `virtualWindow: { viewportHeight, scrollOffset, onScroll }`: rows keep one height except the unfolded one, so every row top is computed without building it; only the rows of the visible band plus 4 overscan rows are built inside its own `scroll_area` (`<prefix>_accordion_scroll`, initial `style.scroll`), and the window is computed by the existing `bevy_panel_virtual_window.js#resolveVirtualWindow`. The scroll handler asks for a new window only when the visible rows leave the built band; `accordionListRowTop(index)` is the offset a panel sets before revealing a row. Without `virtualWindow` the list is unchanged. `bevy_panel_contact_runtime.js` shows the current user's row first (`contact_model.js#currentUserEntry` from the resolved snapshot), then the whole book (`collectPeopleDirectory({ current })` reads the public directory and the local book in parallel, one collator sort); its list window and `contact.list.scroll` intent live in panel state, and select-all covers every selectable row, not only the built ones. `bevy_panel_comm_runtime.js`/`bevy_panel_comm_inbox_runtime.js` window the inbox (`comm.inbox.scroll`). `bevy_panel_calendar_runtime.js` no longer awaits its read in `onOpen` (the panel mounts at once) and reads only the displayed range (`projectCalendarRange` start/end passed to the existing service range filter, which keeps recurring masters and exceptions); reads are serialised and coalesced, and any intent that moves the range triggers one. Evidence: `temp/accordion_list_window.probe.mjs` (geometry checked with the real layout/scroll engines), `temp/large_lists_panels.probe.mjs` (real Web app, 2000 vCard contacts and 2101 ICS events).

2026-10-09 Ordered backdrop shader readiness: `atome/renderers/bevy-core/src/workspace_blur_composition.rs` leaves compilation to Bevy’s existing pipeline queue. The shared transparent pass waits for queued/creating pipelines and temporarily unavailable shader imports before drawing a capture group; permanent shader compilation errors remain explicit. No synchronous cache processing during bind-group preparation, new renderer, public API, camera, material, mutation owner or native boundary. `tests/rendering/backdrop_composition.rs` exercises shader removal/reinsertion and identical recovered GPU pixels.

The canonical fixed row composes system material, passive depth paint and name/symbol tints without reading object fills. project_view_surface_tree.js composes the root rail; project_view_footer.js removes Back and its column and reuses the canonical fixed-row geometry. project_view_list_branch_color.js is deleted after its only production consumer and obsolete assertions are removed. Panel text editing/layout/hidden service, context menu resolver and shared preview capture remain their existing owners. eVe/intuition/eVeIntuition.js exposes its existing ensureMysticContextItemsRuntime loader to the shared panel text-editing owner; the loader installs the existing context runtime. eVe/domains/matrix/matrix_template_runtime.js uses its existing notice/error projection for clipboard refusals.

Programme shows its identity; Monitor shows a real sleep summary or localized No data. program_monitor_sources.js reads consented recorded measurements as well as declarations and the native health source. matrix_template_runtime.js binds icon_button declarations to the existing panel button and drains late queued renders; matrix_module_runtime.js includes root geometry in its existing template signature. 2026-10-06 Mystic expertise level: `eVe/intuition/runtime/eve_intuition/main_menu_content_runtime.js` adds the `level` palette (`children: level_beginner/intermediate/advanced`) to the existing `utilities` children plus the three `ui.level.beginner|intermediate|advanced` leaves (`extra_input.mastery_level`); `eVe/intuition/tools/user_mastery_level_preference.js` owns the write of the existing `preferences.visual.masteryLevel`; `tool_runtime_bootstrap.js` registers the three handlers and `tool_runtime_bootstrap_defs_b.js` declares the three tool defs; `mystic_context_items_runtime.js` marks the active choice with the existing `check`/`user` icons. `intuition/shared/tool_family.js` classifies the three keys as `system`.

2026-10-06 First launch uses ordinary Matrix system templates. Canonical assembly declarations: eVe/domains/templates/first_launch_template_catalog.js; installer and linked duplication/sync: system_template_runtime.js and project_template_runtime.js. eVe/domains/matrix/matrix_template_runtime.js composes existing panel controls and Dashboard factories on shared Bevy projection; access v2 uses `dashboard_card_records.js` content glass and complete text. It subscribes to the canonical surface-size owner and keeps centred animation records outside the scrolling cells. `eVe/intuition/tools/user_login_visual_contract.js` owns the original light timings; `user_login_light_runtime.js` reuses the texture resolver, record-in-layout owner and tree-motion path. `atome/renderers/bevy-core/src/login_light.rs` and `assets/shaders/login_light.wgsl` implement the authorized light material in reserved procedural mode 4; other modes remain in their existing owner. `tests/rendering/login_light.rs` covers resident material/texture behavior. eVe/domains/user/first_launch_runtime.js coordinates the existing profile, auth and project owners. dashboard_basic_runtime.js composes clock, weather, programme and health owners. The obsolete DOM login views and canvas-pixel painter remain removed; user_login_sequence.js remains the single entry. Central skins and system tokens are unchanged.

2026-10-06 Couleur panel live preview during a palette sweep: `domains/rendering/bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js#setPaletteHover` now emits a dedicated `palette_preview` / `palette_preview_leave` pair, and only during a held palette gesture, so a control can show the candidate under the finger without reintroducing panel hover paint; `trackPaletteHover` re-announces the same target on every move (`follow`) so the preview tracks the finger instead of freezing on the entry colour. `intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_color_control.js` stores the pre-gesture `previewBase`, applies the pointed value and calls a new `onPreview(channels, { phase })` hook; the wheel/bar captures and `colorSwatchGridNode` declare the preview pair next to their `palette_choose`, and the wheel preview is bounded to the painted circle so the square's corners count as a leave. `intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_color_runtime.js` bridges `onPreview` to `intuition/tools/selection_style_apply.js#previewColorOnSelection` / `clearSelectionColorPreview` (lazy import, and `onClose` clears any lingering preview only when one was shown). The ephemeral paint routes through the existing direct-style owner: `domains/rendering/project_scene_runtime.js#previewProjectSceneRecordStyles` -> `project_scene_direct_style_runtime.js#tryApplyDirectEphemeralStyles` accepts a `fill` patch and repaints the projected node without touching the record, so the release stays the single committed write and leaving the colour element puts the object's own colour back. Evidence: `tests/eve/bevy_panel_color_control_contract.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_panel_sweep_contract.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_projection_adapter_contract.test.mjs`.

2026-10-06 Couleur panel control repair: `intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_color_control.js` raises the generated hue/saturation wheel to 768 px with a two-texel rim fade, hides the duplicate heading through a `showTitle` option while keeping the accessible label, clamps the brightness/opacity handle inside the track (`[0, width-knob]`) and reuses the shared `buttonMaterial.dot`/`knobShadow`, and makes the painted bar and its `pointer_capture` both absolute in the stack so a drag on the visible track reaches the capture. The wheel and bar captures declare their own `palette_choose`; `intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_projection.js#decoratePanelSweepTree` now honors a control-declared `palette_choose`, forwards the local release event and still closes the surface, so a quick-mode release over the wheel or a bar chooses the pointed value once. Evidence: `tests/eve/bevy_panel_color_control_contract.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_panel_sweep_contract.test.mjs`.

2026-10-06 Persistent profile wallpaper: navigation preserves the existing media URL, hidden video decoder, frame callbacks and GPU wallpaper entity. The canonical project colour is projected as RGBA `cover` above the wallpaper and below every Atome, transparent for Dashboard and foreground full-surface Dashboard Matrix. The existing surface-background event/export carries this cover; no DOM layer, additional player, registry or durable property is added. Cover-only updates keep resident image/video resources; resize updates the video mesh in place. External video queue ordering uses the extracted mesh transform, matching sprites and preserving the reserved background depths.

2026-10-07 Browser outbox refusals: rows the server refuses (row-specific verdict) move to the IndexedDB `refused_queue` store (DB v3) — kept, reported by `squirrel:workspace-sync-refused`, counted as unsent by `importWorkspaceStates` (owner rows only, through the `owner_created` index; records and events are likewise read within the `<owner>:` key range), carried by adoption — and return to the outbox on `squirrel:remote-session-ready` (`requeueRefusedBrowserWorkspaceEvents`). Transport failures and authentication refusals stop the pass in place. Video wallpaper idle freeze: `bevy_surface_background_runtime.js` pauses the wallpaper after 10 s without pointer/touch/wheel/key activity or while hidden, and resumes on the next gesture.
2026-10-07 Browser outbox read path: `guest_workspace_store.js` (IndexedDB `squirrel_guest_workspace_v1`, now version 2) indexes the `queue` store by `[owner_id, created_at]` (`owner_created`). `pendingWorkspaceEvents` reads that index (same rows and order as the former filter+sort) and `oldestPendingWorkspaceEvent` returns only the next row; `browser_workspace.js` sends oldest-first one row at a time and stops on a refused row exactly as before. Previously every commit deserialized the whole outbox. Media source passthrough: `media_source.js#isPassthroughMediaSource` (blob/data/virtual/file/assets heads) short-circuits route normalization before any full-string work; `readMediaUserId` parses a URL only when the string can carry `media_user_id`.
2026-10-06 Browser local access projection: atome/src/squirrel/apis/unified/adole_api/browser_workspace.js owns the transient capabilities returned by local state-current/atome get and list. It verifies the session owner namespace and retains existing server projections. guest_workspace_store.js remains the durable IndexedDB owner. The persistent IndexedDB probe covers the actual Atome reader, owner isolation and session changes.

2026-10-06 Project Template selector: eVe/intuition/tools/project_templates.js composes the native panel shell/selectable list, listProjectTemplates, ensureSystemTemplate and ordinary project activation. The project-background sidebar exposes project_templates first; Dashboard Pro is the existing dashboard system template at version 3, with its existing Matrix and interactions. New Template creates and marks an ordinary project. The canonical bootstrap declares ui.project.templates.panel/open/new.

2026-10-05 Authorized Matrix/Interaction extensions. `eVe/domains/matrix/matrix_grid_model.js` validates rows/columns/gap/headers; `matrix_grid_runtime.js` composes ordinary group/shape/text trees through the standard creator and applies exact frames through commitRuntimeSetBatch. `matrix_grid_projection.js` owns frame retention and excludes surplus cells from presentation without deleting their canonical contents. Native settings live in `intuition/tools/matrix_objects.js`; shared panel-open options in `runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_panel_open_options.js` serve both Interaction and `tools/panel_open.js`. Matrix/panel command declarations and handlers moved into bootstrap_matrix_defs/handlers; binding declarations remain with bootstrap_midi_schemas. Interaction tool tiles/catalog moved into bevy_panel_interaction_tools without copying their builders. Four persistent suites cover creation, relayout, native editor preservation and pointer hold.

2026-10-04 Dark panel skin: `eVe/elements/skin/panel_skin.js` owns both the approved panel presentation and the retained workspace preset, using the existing system unit. Canonical panel owners consume it; `bevy_panel_footer.js` and `bevy_panel_hierarchy.js` split shell composition without a second shell/navigation engine. Existing virtual-window, menu-palette and projection-surface responsibilities are extracted into their canonical modules. `atome/renderers/bevy-core/src/surface_paint.rs`, `shape_texture.rs` and `render_style_ops.rs` extend the existing silhouette cache/style flow for optional gradients, edge colours and inset blur. Active inventory: `eVe/documentations/panel_html_inventory.md`; actual acceptance: `eVe/documentations/dark_panel_skin_validation_2026-10-04.md`.

2026-10-04 Tauri black login repair: `auth_methods_session_account.js#tryAutoLogin` settles failed desktop recovery through `clearSessionState`; `eVe/eVe.js` starts the canonical user background before intuition bootstrap, avoiding the first-login-pixels/deferred-background cycle. Evidence: native Tauri video + login choices, auth restart and wallpaper contracts.

# Atome / eVe Code Map

2026-10-11 Contact footer search: `bevy_panel_contact_runtime.js` owns the ephemeral query and composes `createPanelTextEditingRuntime` and the existing Squirrel `contacts/service_contact_utils.js#matchesQuery`; `bevy_panel_contact_actions.js` composes `textInputNode` and its native field handlers alongside Import/Export and account creation. Search resets the virtual-list offset, covers public display names and existing contact fields, and releases its editing session on closure. Web guest pointer/keyboard checks pass at desktop and portrait widths; favorite tint repair and authenticated Dashboard acceptance remain pending authorization.

2026-10-11 Contact conversations (partial): `eVe/intuition/tools/contact_favorites_preference.js` composes confirmed profile preference lists; the contact accordion composes the canonical Bevy icon button, and Dashboard adapters filter stable favorites. `server/wsCommunicationOperations.js` owns authenticated direct-message/conversation/Visio dispatch; `server/communication_messages.js` and `database/communication_messages.js` own participant message Atomes and pagination. `server/visio_commands.js` arbitrates the shared 15-second attempt, and `eVe/domains/visio/communication_call_session.js` composes the existing call and capture owners. Global recording destination extends the factored `media_persistence_service.js`. The central conversation panel and account-media reader integration are not implemented; actual UI and two-account runtime acceptance remain To verify.
2026-10-11 Quick contact panel: `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_contact_conversation_runtime.js` (state, call session, held capture, text/attachments) and `bevy_panel_contact_conversation_view.js` (identity card, live capture visual, review preview, fixed footer with text field and visio/audio/attach icon buttons: tap = call, hold = record); registered by `eVe/intuition/tools/contact_conversation_panel.js`. Dashboard `openContactItem` opens it instead of the address book. Dashboard data: session identity by user id, stale-while-revalidate lanes, favorites-signature contact reload, appointment filter `isDashboardAppointment` in `dashboard_environment.js`. Contacts store: single-record writes and one-time suppressed-contact recovery (`known-bug-solutions/contacts-lost-by-global-replace`).
2026-10-12 Quick contact opens from its Dashboard tile through `createPanelCellPresentation('contact_conversation')` (same as health detail; shared `dashboardCellPresentationFor(state, hit)` in `dashboard_bevy_ui_action_handlers.js`), closes back to it (`requestClose`), and is closed with the Dashboard or on header tap (`closeContactItem`, `fromCellOnly`). History view `bevy_panel_contact_conversation_history.js` (bubbles, older page, reveal of the last message) reads `communication.conversation.read` through the injected `readConversation`.
2026-10-13 Quick contact takes: `bevy_panel_contact_conversation_takes.js` (hold/drag-to-lock/settle via `updateTreeMotion`, `state.takes[]`, Retake/Remove, playback) composed by the runtime (`sendAll`: one message per take then text + attachments). Preview zone replaces the history while recording/reviewing. `eVe/domains/media/record_preview_playback.js` replays a record inside a UI preview (shared audio executor + Bevy decoder of the projected record, request flagged `presentation`).

2026-10-03 Social sharing (TikTok, Instagram, Facebook). Shared contract and pure planning: `atome/src/squirrel/social/contracts.js` (networks, routes, statuses, documented media limits, `social.*` commands) and `capabilities.js` (route planning, media signature sniffing); device side `client.js` (provider-socket requests, official page opening per host, share sheet, export via `shared/file_export.js`). Server owner `server/social/`: `social_operations.js` (dispatcher, reached from `server/wsAiProviderOperations.js` for `social.*`), `social_sessions.js`, `social_oauth.js` (attempts + the two provider-imposed HTTP routes, registered in `server/server.js`), `social_media.js` (ffprobe/ffmpeg variants, reuses `server_media.js` transcode), `social_jobs.js`, `social_http.js`, adapters `social_tiktok.js`/`social_instagram.js`/`social_facebook.js`. Tokens are `social.<network>` records of `server/providerCredentialVault.js` (new `storeRecord/readRecord/removeRecord`, `socialRoot`). `server/auth_secret_config.js` now owns one dev secret-file loader for SMS and social config. UI: `bevy_panel_home_social.js` (Préférences › Communication), `bevy_panel_comm_social.js` + `bevy_panel_comm_social_view.js` (Communication). Runtime/MCP: `eVe/intuition/tools/social_share.js`; `atome/src/squirrel/atome/mcp_handlers_contracts.js` replaces `mcp_handlers_tv.js` (one factory for TV and social). iOS `export_file_share` in `NativePersonalImport.swift`; Tauri opener plugin initialised in `lib.rs`. Doc: `atome/documentations/social_sharing.md`.

2026-10-03 YouTube/TV Finder repair (acceptance incomplete). The existing Finder composes textInputNode/panelTextFieldHandlers with createPanelTextEditingRuntime; bevy_panel_finder_runtime.js owns query/context/loading, and bevy_panel_finder_interactions.js contains its extracted record/selection/transfer intentions. YouTube reads the canonical WS data envelope and pagination. Both media tools remove their ribbon input consumers. The existing bevy_video_stream_source_runtime.js prefers native HLS and loads the packaged Hls.js decoder for MSE, with cancellation and destruction in the same lifecycle; scripts/bundle-hls.js packages the official pinned dependency and license. YouTube IFrame identity, readiness, autoplay errors and late-selection cancellation remain in youtube_tool_runtime.js. Media messages reuse the existing EN/FR input-tool dictionaries; interaction dictionaries remain below 500 lines. Provider provisioning and real playback are still open.

2026-10-03 Health association follow-up: dashboard_health_modules.js now awaits status before the first mounted panel tree and places only width-bounded actions in the shared footer. Associate remains available without checked measures. tests/eve/dashboard_health_authorization.test.mjs covers first-mount timing, footer bounds, account/seal gates and the native request path. Removed unused Health help translations.

2026-10-03 HealthKit authorization: Dashboard header entry and explicit association initialize read access centrally through the existing health owner/channel. iOS checkbox and tile actions never request HealthKit; Android and Motion retain their selected-type flow. Native capability discovery covers all 33 catalog monitors; authorization excludes Motion, checks request necessity, and reports unknown/error states. No native command was added. Late pre-authorization reads are discarded and re-read; failed requests no longer report success. Values remain memory-only and outside logs, DOM, history and sync.

2026-10-02 Personal imports now have one canonical owner (`atome/src/squirrel/shared/canonical_import.js`) and collector (`atome/src/squirrel/shared/import_lifecycle.js`). Contacts local storage and duplicate ICS/recurrence implementations are replaced; native Apple history, DAV continuation, media photos and explicit file exports reuse existing owners. Implementation and acceptance limits: `eVe/documentations/PERSONAL_IMPORT_VALIDATION_2026-10-02.md`.

2026-10-12 Native contacts imports use the stable key `macos_contacts`; former `macos_contacts/<surface>` origins are adopted in `canonical_import.js` and the copies they left are removed by `contacts/local_source.js` (`removeFormerImportCopies`) after a complete read. See `known-bug-solutions/contacts-import-interrupted-and-duplicated`.
2026-10-12 New-device restore: `guest_workspace_store.js#importWorkspaceStates` flattens server states through `atome_record_projection.js#mapStateCurrentToAtome` (before, a fresh browser restored nothing). Business identity for imports lives in `canonical_import.js` (`identityKeys`/`mergeIntoExisting`); contacts (`local_source.js`) key by phone, native events (`calendar/import_source.js`) by series + start. See `known-bug-solutions/new-device-restores-nothing`.
2026-10-12 Dashboard data: `dashboard_contact_items.js` (contacts population, favorites row, one tile per person) and `dashboard_data_change_subscriptions.js` (directory/calendar/project change triggers) are extracted from `dashboard_data_adapters.js` / `dashboard_data_controller.js`. The controller caches `calendar` per account, shares in-flight reads, skips unchanged repaints and exposes `promoteLeavingProject`. See `known-bug-solutions/home-panel-save-erases-favorites`, `dashboard-transition-jump`.

2026-10-15 Audio to finalize (todo/audio_to_finalize.md). Shared native audio crate `atome/engines/audio-engine` (Tauri re-exports it; iOS links it through `platforms/ios/bevy-renderer/src/audio.rs`, C ABI `atome_ios_audio_command` → `commands.rs#dispatch`; Swift `Common/NativeKiraEngine.swift`, `application/AppNativeKiraEngine.swift`, AUv3 `auv3/AUv3KiraEngine.swift`); `output_idle.rs` (output at rest), `host_output.rs` (AUv3 host-pulled output), `calibration.rs` (`audio_measure_input_latency`). `atome/engines/mix-graph`: `voice.rs` (VoiceEnvelope: clip fades, cell attack/release, frame-counted), `voice_control.rs` (late end of a voice: `audio_set_voice_end`), `delay.rs` (time-modulable delay), `resample.rs` (live input). Cells and Scenes: kernel `scene_schema.js`, `scenes.js`, `cell_content.js`, `takes.js` (comping, `clip.take`), `clip_join.js` (`clip.join`); transport `core/media_engine/molecule_cell_player.js` + `tools/molecule/runtime_cells.js`, `runtime_cell_recording.js`; view `project_view_timeline_{scenes,cells,cell_gestures,cells_rail,cells_midi,clip_rail,primitives}.js`. Input calibration `recording/input_calibration.js`. Module loader dedupe by id (`atome/src/utils/module_loader_runtime.js`). A/V: `bevy_video_clock_follow.js` (picture follows the sound clock), `MediaPlaybackAudioExecutor#outputLatencySeconds`. Mixer: `molecule_mix_fader_actions.js`, `layoutMixRow({ handedness })`; pointer runtime `long_press.quickTravelScrolls`. Kira backend helpers `atome/src/application/audio_runtime/kira_backend_support.js`.
2026-10-14 Timeline v1.6 first draft (todo/timeline/timeline_cellules_cahier_des_charges_v1.6.md). The project timeline is owned by the project (root Molecule): `project_view_timeline_owner.js` resolves/opens/seeds it; `runtime_deletion.js#isProjectTimelineOwner` keeps it. View = BevyUI tree `project_view_timeline_{content,layout,tree,gestures,rail,recording}.js`; rec. tools drop out of the ribbon (`bevy_ui_main_menu_tool_drag_out.js`, `drop_payload`) as a 4-bar Record region. Sample-accurate playback and overdub: Web engine `platforms/web/audio-wasm/src/scheduled_backend.rs` (Kira backend owning the AudioContext, blocks split at due frames, base segments per discontinuity) and native `atome/engines/audio-engine/src/scheduled_backend.rs` (shared crate since 2026-10-14) (render thread ahead of cpal into a ringbuf FIFO, host-instant anchors) + `playback_schedule.rs` (region normalisation now in `atome/engines/mix-graph/src/voice.rs`); `eVe/core/media_engine/molecule_render_schedule.js` schedules every voice of a playback in one engine call; `recording/exact_placement.js` places a take (Web: shared context frame + round trip; native: `capture_clock` host instants) minus the input calibration (`media_input_devices.js#readInputCalibrationSeconds`, `av.latency` scope `calibration`). Recorder worklet ignores lagging `currentFrame`. Metronome: `runtime_metronome.js` (click track, count-in). Input tool: `bevy_panel_media_input_runtime.js` + `media_input_devices.js` (Web deviceId, native device name). Old `mol:*` scene retired.

2026-10-14 Prompteur (all lots). A prompteur is a `text` Atome carrying the `prompter` property (schema in `atome/src/shared/core_atome_types.js`: version, source_id, mode manual|duration|keypoints, between scroll|page, clock project|host, offset_seconds, weight_by_length, cues[{key,at_seconds,keypoint,locked}]; time on screen `playback_dwell_seconds`). Owners under `eVe/intuition/tools/prompter/`: `prompter_model.js` (property, lines, reading duration), `prompter_timing.js` (pure: line instants per mode, cue keys that follow edits, cascade/stretch/record merge, reading position through the Molecule automation evaluator), `prompter_actions.js` (single writer: one commit per edit, transport, subtitles export, Molecule clip resize), `prompter_clock.js` (project transport or AUv3 host clock), `prompter_display_preferences.js` (`preferences.visual.prompter`), `prompter_subtitles.js` (SRT/VTT), `prompter_tools.js` (generator `text.prompteur`, commands). Panel: `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_prompter_runtime.js` (state, clock → motion patches, recording) + `_operator_view.js` + `_reading_view.js`. Shared bricks created: `atome/src/squirrel/shared/timecode.js` (format/parse, bars.beats, beat quantize), `evaluateAutomationKeyframes` in `molecule/kernel/automation.js`, `eVe/intuition/tools/core/text_lines.js` (the line rule, leaf), `eVe/intuition/tools/user_visual_preference_writer.js` (visual preference writer, also used by the mastery level), `atome/src/application/audio_runtime/auv3_host_bridge.js` (the JS end of `sendBridgeJSON`, installed by `audio.facade.js`). Timeline: `molecule/render/timeline_previews.js` draws one tick per line on a prompteur clip. i18n: `eVe/i18n/languages_prompter.js`.

2026-09-28 (correction 4) The centre hold now belongs to a finger that actually stayed on the centre, and the BevyUI lane under the Flower stays visible. Three owners. (1) `eVe/intuition/ribbon/bevy_ui_mystic_motion.js`: `createMysticCenterHold` takes its duration and tolerance from `RIBBON_TOKENS.mysticCenterHoldMs` (3000 ms) and `RIBBON_TOKENS.mysticCenterHoldTolerancePx` (10 px), both new in `eVe/intuition/ribbon/tokens.js` beside `mysticAutoOpenDwellMs`; `holdAnchor()` (`state.openPoint || state.rootPoint`) and `strayedFromCentre(x, y)` give one measurement shared by `startCentreHold` and `moveCentreHold`, and `startCentreHold` now refuses to arm when the live point the runtime reports is already beyond that tolerance — the asynchronous `openAt` (context resolution, item loading, tile mounting) used to start the 3 s countdown wherever the finger happened to be, with no later movement left to cancel it; `pump` scales the centre by `holdDurationMs` instead of a literal 3000. (2) `eVe/intuition/ribbon/bevy_ui_mystic_runtime.js` keeps `state.pointerPoint`, the live position: `updateHover` records it (with `state.lastPoint`) before its readiness gate, `openAt` only seeds it when nothing livelier arrived, `resetState` clears it, and the end of the opening re-reads it once the tiles are mounted (`updateHover({ clientX, clientY, dwell: true })`), so the tile a finger stopped on during the cascade highlights and arms its dwell instead of staying mute until the finger moves again. (3) `eVe/domains/rendering/bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js` is back to `HEAD`: correction 3's blanket `isMysticPointerInteractionActive` guard in `pointermove` starved every BevyUI hit test while the Flower held the pointer, which is what made the record tool invisible. `eVe/domains/rendering/surface_interaction_runtime.js` keeps the mystic-aware `stopSurfaceEvent` for `pointermove`/`pointerup` and adds the stale-session reset (`if (pointerMatches(uiPointerSession, event)) uiPointerSession = null;`, before the interceptor) found while validating: a release swallowed by the Flower left the surface session open on that pointer and the next canvas drag produced `drag.start` and nothing else. Evidence: `node temp/mystic_assistant_hold_probe.mjs` (moves a finger 63 px onto record before `openAt` resolves and then holds still: the hold reports the stray, hover is `record`, the palette opens and the assistant stays `[]`; a deliberate centre click after the released press opens it), `node --test tests/eve/bevy_ui_mystic_contract.probe.mjs tests/eve/mystic_drag_boundary_contract.probe.mjs tests/eve/mystic_assistant_gesture.probe.mjs` 26/26 (new test: *a finger that already left the centre while the menu was opening never arms the hold*), `npx vitest run tests/eve/mystic_release_dwell_fling.test.mjs` and the temp mystic regression probes (media hold, hold hover, mouse hover, release/dwell/fling 13/13, drag-behind-menu lanes).

2026-09-28 Apple Photos is the picture source of the import picker, the public directory follows a new account, and the Contact list can create one. `eVe/intuition/runtime/project_media_import_runtime.js#requestImportFilesFromNativeBridge` no longer sends an empty `fileTypes` for every request: an image-only `accept` resolves to the `photos` token, an undeclared `accept` (empty or `*`) resolves to the `*` token, and every other vocabulary keeps the document list. `platforms/ios/atome-auv3/Common/PhotoLibraryImportPicker.swift` (new) owns the Apple Photos surface — a `PHPickerViewController` filtered to `.images`, out of process and requiring no library authorization, sequential `NSItemProvider` reads with the orientation baked into the delivered pixels (PNG when transparency is kept, JPEG otherwise) — and answers the exact `{files:[{name,base64}]}` shape the document picker answers; `FileSystemBridgeOperations.swift#handleLoadFilesWithDocumentPicker` routes the `photos` token to it, the `*` token to `presentImportSourceChoice` (a Photos/Fichiers `UIAlertController` whose cancel keeps the "User cancelled" cancellation shape) and everything else to `iCloudFileManager`, and the `multiple` flag the web layer always sent is now honoured by both pickers (`allowsMultipleSelection`). `server/wsPhoneLinkAuth.js` asks the public-directory projection for a refresh (`refreshDirectoryProfile` → `DirectoryPublicService.refreshPrincipal`) after `update-user`, after a successful `session-delete-account` and after an authentication action that emits a token, so a newly created phone account lands in the projection and its `directory.public` event reaches the Contact/Communication list without restarting the app. The Contact panel gains a `Create user` button (`bevy_panel_contact_actions.js` → `contact.create_user` → `bevy_panel_contact_runtime.js#createUser`) that loads the Home tool module and calls the new `window.open_create_user_panel` global (`intuition/tools/user.js` → `user_home_panel_runtime.js#openCreateUser` → `user_login_sequence.js#openCredentials`), the phone step of the one shared sequence (`enterCredentials` extracted from the choice callback) — no second registration path. Evidence: `tests/probes/project_media_import_native_picker_contract.probe.mjs` 1/1, `tests/probes/phone_link_directory_projection.probe.mjs` 1/1, `tests/probes/contact_create_user_account_flow.probe.mjs` 1/1, `tests/eve/bevy_panel_contact_pick_contract.test.mjs` 7/7, `npm run check:syntax` 2,251 files, `npm run check:m0` green.

2026-09-28 Mystic dwell token and the project-view Visual hand-over: the single auto-open duration now lives beside every other ribbon gesture threshold in `RIBBON_TOKENS.mysticAutoOpenDwellMs` (`eVe/intuition/ribbon/tokens.js`) at 750 ms — half of the 1500 ms literal it replaces — and `MYSTIC_AUTO_OPEN_DWELL_MS` in `eVe/intuition/ribbon/bevy_ui_mystic_runtime.js` reads it, so the held lane and the free-hover lane keep one shared, tunable value. `eVe/domains/rendering/project_view_visual_interaction_runtime.js` hands the held gesture over to the Flower the long press opened: `longPress` marks the session `handedToMenu`, `drag` answers `cancelled` without emitting anything, `release` forces its cancel path, and the existing BevyUI `window.eveBevyUiRuntime.cancelPointerGesture()` releases the captured pointer — the media preview no longer travels behind the open menu. Evidence: `tests/eve/project_view_visual_mystic_handover.test.mjs` (new, 2 tests), `tests/eve/mystic_release_dwell_fling.test.mjs` 10/10, `node temp/mystic_release_dwell_fling_integration_probe.mjs` 13/13, `npm run check:syntax` 2248 files, `npm run check:m0` green.

2026-09-28 (correction 2) The held click belongs to the open Flower on every route. The project-view Visual hand-over above was still insufficient: a menu opened by a press the canvas never followed (a BevyUI node such as the Visual preview, a list or Matrix item, a text field) ran `openMysticContextMenu` with no `runtimeState.pointer` session, so `setMysticPointerLock` had never been taken for that pointer and `isMysticPointerInteractionActive(pointerId)` stayed `false` at every guard, letting the held pointer drag the media under the menu. `eVe/intuition/mystic/context.js` is now the single owner of that guarantee: `runtimeState.primaryPresses` remembers every primary press (keyed by pointer, carrying the press point, 12 s TTL), `claimHeldPointerForMenu(openingPointer)` — called by `openMysticContextMenu` before `openMysticMenu` — keeps the pointer of a session in `holding`, or otherwise the press still down, in `runtimeState.menuHeldPointer` and takes the existing `setMysticPointerLock` (source `mystic.menu.held_press`) whose `eve:mystic-pointer-takeover` the surface session, the atome drag/resize runtimes and `drag_runtime.js` already watch; `onPointerMove` keeps routing `updateHover({ allowActivation: false, dwell: true })` to the menu and then cancels the move for every owner below, `dragstart`/`selectstart` are cancelled the same way, `lostpointercapture` no longer ends a `holding` session, `finishMenuHeldPress` returns exactly one `releaseAt` (same 48 px radial rule as the canvas route) and `onPointerTerminal` hands the pointer back after the canvas route closed its own session, so the lock never outlives the press. `eVe/domains/rendering/bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js` cancels a `pointermove` gesture for a locked pointer; `eVe/domains/rendering/surface_runtime.js` rolls a drag back to its origin on a `mystic.*` end. Evidence: `tests/eve/mystic_release_dwell_fling.test.mjs` 13/13 (three new tests, red before the fix), `tests/eve/project_view_visual_mystic_handover.test.mjs` 2/2, the four Mystic probes, `npm run check:syntax` 2,252 files, `npm run check:m0` green.
2026-09-28 (correction 3) A right-click keeps its two fingers to itself, exactly like a held primary press. Two owners changed. `eVe/domains/rendering/surface_interaction_runtime.js` arms an atome gesture only for the primary button now (`isPrimaryPointerButton`: `button === 0`, or `-1` on touch/pen): a secondary or auxiliary press never begins a drag, a resize or a crop on the surface, it belongs to the context menu, and the guard sits after the interceptor, the lasso and the Mystic lock so those keep seeing the event. `eVe/intuition/mystic/context.js` replaces its single press ledger with one `createPressLedger()` identity contract (key, pointer, press point, `PRESS_TTL_MS` = 12 s, `pressKeyOf`) instantiated twice: `runtimeState.primaryPresses` and the new `runtimeState.secondaryPresses`, filled by `onPointerDown` for `button === 2`; `heldMenuPress(pointerId)` (primary first, then secondary) becomes the single source for `claimHeldPointerForMenu`, the open-menu branch of `onPointerMove` (`updateHover({ allowActivation: false, dwell: true })` then cancellation), `finishMenuHeldPress` (radial travel measured from the right-click point) and `mysticOwnsHeldPointer` (`dragstart`/`selectstart`); `onPointerTerminal` forgets both ledgers, `onContextMenu` on an already-open menu releases the held pointer, and `cancelForDragStart`/cleanup clear both. Evidence: `tests/eve/mystic_release_dwell_fling.test.mjs` 16/16 (three new tests: the Flower opened by a right-click keeps the two fingers, a second right-click closes it and hands the pointer back, a menu opened without a held press claims nothing) and `node temp/mystic_contextmenu_drag_behind_menu_probe.mjs` (no `drag.move` while the two fingers glide, no late surface session; its first assertion fails with the button guard removed).

2026-09-28 One door for the bare surface during held gestures: `eVe/domains/rendering/gesture_presentation_hold.js` owns the presentation hold atom drags already had — the selection-overlay flag from `project_scene_selection_state.js` (read by every projection, so no frame rendered mid-gesture can bring the frame back) plus `gesture_chrome_suspension.js` for ribbon, rail and panels, which still restores only what it suspended. The hold token rides on the gesture (a pointer session, or the view gesture's own object), so the object pinch that takes over a drag session inherits it and a single release settles both; the first gesture masks, the last one reveals. `surface_interaction_runtime.js` keeps the atom drag on that door and publishes `canvas.__eveSurfaceIntentDispatch` for gestures without a session; `surface_pinch_runtime.js` holds at the first real movement and releases on every exit (pointer up, cancel, Escape, blur, 160 ms wheel idle), and follows the drag's selection rule: a moving gesture never selects. The pinch previously selected the atome at the second contact (`select` dispatched in `begin` by `4e6bfa0a`), so the frame and its shadow appeared during gesture detection, the bare surface hid them, and the release restored them on an unselected atome. The pending touch is now carried by `session.tapSelect` and dispatched from `finish()` only when the contacts did not move; the trackpad wheel (ctrl+wheel) never selects. `project_view_gesture_runtime.js` holds for the two-finger pinch, the Safari gestures and the wheel (zoom as well as pan) and settles everything on blur/visibilitychange. Locks abandoned by a replaced document are settled when a surface opens a new session. Evidence: `temp/pinch_select_trace_probe.mjs` (11/11), `temp/gesture_presentation_hold_probe.mjs` (44/44), `temp/gesture_chrome_suspension_probe.mjs`, `temp/gesture_chrome_esm_link_probe.mjs`, `temp/view_pinch_lock_probe.mjs`; `tests/eve` failure set identical to HEAD; journal `todo/project_view_zoom.md`.

2026-09-27 Auth persistence/pre-send owners: `auth_methods_login.js#localRequest` routes iOS local session describe/challenge/resume directly through native IPC, while `WebViewManagerIPC.swift` and `WebViewManagerScriptMessages.swift` implement and origin-gate `auth_local_request`; desktop `nativeKey` sends the complete Tauri command payload, including explicit null Option fields. `dashboard_news_modules.js#bootstrap` starts GPS only for an already-granted permission and leaves the explicit locate control as prompt owner; `application/Info.plist` describes that weather-only location use. Evidence: `tests/eve/auth_native_restart_contract.test.mjs`, `tests/probes/dashboard_weather_runtime.test.mjs` and the physical-iPhone boot log recorded in Framework State.

2026-09-27 Guest blank-screen recovery owner: `eVe/intuition/tools/user_home_panel_runtime.js#enterAnonymousWorkspace` bounds both `AdoleAPI.security.startGuest` and the canonical `openWorkspace` handoff, reports the exact failed stage, clears partial workspace DOM through the existing injected cleanup owner, and resets the shared login sequence. `tests/probes/guest_workspace_failure_recovers_login.probe.mjs` covers immediate rejection, stalled presentation and stalled guest creation.

2026-09-27 Cross-device handoff owners: `auth_phone_link_client.js#consumeLink` distinguishes the matching local attempt (signed direct consume) from a foreign attempt (token-only `phone-link-confirm`, no session install). `auth_phone_link.js#confirm` is the transactional token verifier and approval state transition. `wsPhoneLinkAuth.js` routes that action and sends `phone-link-ready` only to sockets carrying the matching `_authLinkAttemptId`; normal signed resume remains the sole session issuance path. Evidence: `auth_phone_link.test.mjs` and `auth_phone_link_client.test.mjs`.

2026-09-27 Guest/Tauri/iOS restart repair: `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/lib.rs#configured_tauri_fastify_url` owns the unconditional production default. `local_auth.rs#start_guest` and `AiSRuntimeAuthAccounts.swift#handleStartGuest` resume IDs authorized by the active guest registry before applying new-ID collision checks; iOS `handleMe` now projects a verified guest without requiring a user Atome. `local_auth_device.rs#describe` and `AiSRuntimeAuthChallenges.swift#localAuthDescriptor` discover unlocked native grants. `auth_methods_login.js#restoreLocalAuthorization` consumes that descriptor and `auth_device.js#forScope` reopens the matching Keychain/Secure Enclave key; Web storage is only a cache. Evidence: `auth_native_restart_contract.test.mjs`, focused auth suites, Rust compilation and the unsigned iOS build.

2026-09-27 Native phone-link runtime repair: `atome/src/squirrel/apis/loadServerConfig.js` and `unified/adole_backend.js` own cross-runtime production Fastify resolution; `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/lib.rs` injects the configured authority before application code, while the JavaScript loader defaults Tauri to production when that injection is missing instead of deriving the local `127.0.0.1:3001` endpoint. An explicit native or persisted developer override remains the only loopback opt-in. The restarted real Tauri WebView was inspected with `https://atome.one` and `wss://atome.one/ws/api`. `atome/src/squirrel/security/auth_device.js` avoids persisted WebCrypto lookup keys when the existing native key bridge is present, `auth_phone_link_client.js` preserves Web Locks callback results, and `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/auth_device.rs` owns the single macOS login-keychain EC identity. `auth_methods_session_account.js` settles failed stored-guest restoration so native boot can present login. `application/SceneDelegate.swift` forwards every Universal Link lifecycle entry to `AuthLinkInbox.swift`, including the cold-launch connection options that were previously discarded. `user_home_panel_runtime.js` treats a failed guest workspace projection as a failure and restores login instead of returning success over a blank canvas. Evidence: focused auth/config suites, `guest_auto_login_failure_settles_boot.probe.mjs`, `guest_workspace_failure_recovers_login.probe.mjs`, Tauri/iOS Debug builds, a real production WebSocket validation response and signed physical-iPhone cold-link intake. Carrier delivery was user-confirmed.

2026-09-27 Phone-link authentication migration (partial; not ready for deployment): server/auth_phone_link.js, auth_link_security.js, auth_sessions.js and wsPhoneLinkAuth.js own persistent proof exchange through /ws/api. AdoleAPI auth_methods_login.js owns remote sessions and independent local grants; auth_phone_link_client.js persists only non-bearer descriptors. `atome/src/shared/phone_number.js` owns permission-free country inference and local-to-E.164 normalization shared by login UI and auth. Tauri local_auth_{accounts,device,tokens}.rs and iOS AiSRuntimeAuth{Accounts,Challenges,Tokens}.swift replace inline password authentication. AuthLinkInbox.swift owns Universal Link intake. browser_workspace.js extends guest_workspace_store.js for identity-scoped events, files and outbox; sw.js/offline_media.js and server/offline_app_assets.js own the separate application cache and local media reads. The pure event contract is at atome/src/shared/adole_event_contract.js. Password/OTP login modules are removed; remaining acceptance gates and evidence are in todo/auth_phone_link_implementation.md.

2026-09-30 Panel quick mode and hover policy: `intuition/tools/core/panel_toggle_rule.js#resolveToolPanelSurface` (taxonomy first, else declared case surface, else `PANEL_SURFACE_DEFINITIONS`, else `buildPanelRuntimeConfigByToolId()`) is the single surface resolution. Ribbon: `bevy_ui_main_menu_runtime.js#invokeToolItem` carries the one activation body (latch bookkeeping + palette hand-off) that the click, the hold and the sweep all call; `panelSurfaceForItem`/`itemOpensPanel` exclude expandable palettes; `revealPanel` invokes only `actionOverride: 'state.on'` and only while the surface is closed, so the reveal opens and never toggles shut; `panelRevealHandlers` returns the same `reveal` for `palette_slide_open` and `long_press`; a tool that already owns its hold (`itemOwnsPanelHold`: `canLongPress === true`) keeps `handleHold` on both routes. Panel: `bevy_panel_runtime.js#decoratePanelSweepTree` derives `palette_choose` once per built tree - or keeps a control-declared one, which receives the local release event (the colour wheel and its bars turn the pointed pixel into a value) - so a quick-mode release applies the control then closes the surface, while removing every `hover`/`hover_leave` handler from the mounted panel/list tree. Panel rows never repaint or rebuild merely because scrolling moves them under a stationary pointer. Rail: `atome_contextual_edit_handlers.js#panelSurface` uses the same helper and installs `palette_slide_open: () => (isPanelOpen(panelSurface) ? null : activateDefinition())`; a `longPressToolId` case (Paste → `ui.paste.panel`) binds `long_press` and `palette_slide_open` to the same `longPressDefinition`. Evidence: the focused hover-free case in `tests/eve/bevy_panel_sweep_contract.test.mjs` passes; real iOS/Tauri acceptance remains open.

2026-09-27 Palette quick mode hit-testing: `bevy_ui_main_menu_palette_motion.js#bevyMainMenuPaletteMotionUpdates` tags each travelling palette child with `hitPosition` (its final position) while `position` keeps the 180/70/120 ms sample; `bevy_ui_tree_motion_runtime.js#syncMotionHitTrees` freezes `state.trees` on that destination and leaves `state.sourceTrees`/the projected records animated; `bevy_ui_runtime.js#mountOrUpdate` accepts `hitTree` and stores it in `state.trees`; `bevy_ui_main_menu_runtime.js` passes `hitTree: baseTree` and publishes the idempotent `palette_slide_open`; `atome_contextual_edit_handlers.js#revealPalette` is idempotent; `bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js` marks the gesture `dragOwned` once a hold reveals the options. Evidence: `tests/eve/bevy_ui_nested_motion.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_ui_main_menu_palette_motion.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_ui_main_menu_contract.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/contextual_rail_active_zone.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_ui_pointer_contract.test.mjs`.

2026-09-26 Project view (zoom/pan), content-framed thumbnails, page formats: the project view is ONE affine (`screen = project × zoom + pan`) owned by `eVe/domains/rendering/project_view_camera.js` (dependency-free maths, per-project state, suspended to identity outside the Natural mode) and `project_view_camera_runtime.js` (pushes the `project_view` op, remembers the view per USER in `preferences.projectViews`, never in the project; 100 %, « Tout voir » inside the free area, text re-raster by zoom step). Rust (`atome/renderers/bevy-core/src/project_view.rs`) applies it as a virtual parent — `GlobalTransform = view ∘ Transform` in PostUpdate after propagation and before visibility — to nodes spawned with `project_space: true` (set by `bevy_projection_adapter.js` from `project_view_space.js#isProjectSpaceSceneId`: atoms, page ghosts, creation preview, draw retouch mask) and their overlays (selection, shadow, waveform, clip proxy); UI, menus, Dashboard and background stay in screen space and the cameras are untouched. Screen→project conversions go through the camera in `surface_runtime.js` (points carry `screen`), `scene_graph.js#hitTestRenderScene` (per-node space), `project_scene_hit_testing.js`, `computeDropBase`, the background rail, Mystic and text creation. Gestures: `project_view_gesture_runtime.js` (two fingers on the background, ctrl/⌘-wheel zoom, wheel pan, Safari gestures) reached through `window.__eveProjectViewGestures` after UI and atom handling; `bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js` no longer lets a stale hover swallow the wheel. Rail: `main_menu_view_zoom_content.js` (Zoom slider %, 100 %, Tout voir) dispatched by `project_background_rail_runtime.js`; `sliderFollowsValue` keeps an idle slider on its source. Thumbnails: `project_preview_content_bounds.js` frames the content (cover page `page_cover` first) at the screen ratio; `intuition/tools/page_cover_tool.js`; page-only commands via `containerKinds` in `context_menu_resolver.js`. Pages: real sizes (A4 794×1123, A3, A5, Letter), orientation from the drag, `page_format`/`page_orientation` persisted; `page_pdf_document.js` + `page_pdf_export.js` (dedicated capture iframe, Tauri `export_file_save` → Downloads). Evidence: `temp/project_view_zoom.probe.mjs`, `temp/project_view_gestures.probe.mjs`, `temp/preview_pages_lot45.probe.mjs`, Rust `project_view` tests; journal `todo/project_view_zoom.md`.

2026-09-26 Assistant dock accessories: `eVe/voice/assistant/assistant_dock.js` keeps one geometry owner (`resolveAssistantDockGeometry`) and one input owner (`createRibbonInlineSearchRuntime` + `buildInlineSearchNodes`), and now feeds it the four squares of the row from `accessoryNodes`: `assistant_prompt_accessory_submit`, `..._history`, `..._level` and `..._microphone`, in the far order `inlineAccessorySlotX` lays out from the free end (conversations, level palette, microphone). The level palette is `buildLevelMenuNodes` — shared `radioGroupNode` inside the shared panel material — anchored on the level slot through the same `inlineAccessorySlotX`, and fed by `levelMenuOptions()` from `OPENAI_MODEL_PROFILES`, never by a second list. Selection goes through `assistant_runtime.js#api.selectLevel`; its refusal (`conversation_busy`) is caught in `chooseLevel` and painted as `assistant_level_menu_notice` while the palette stays open, and `assistantErrorMessage()` (`assistant_conversation_view.js`) is now the single error-sentence reader for that notice and the conversation block alike. The conversations square drives `historyVisible` and the reveal anchor of `buildAssistantHistoryNodes`; the microphone square drives `api.listen()` and `api.focusText()`. Evidence: `tests/eve/assistant_visual_contract.test.mjs`.

2026-09-26 Assistant without an AI provider: `eVe/voice/assistant/assistant_ai_settings.js` is the single owner of the configuration answer — `AI_SETTINGS_ERROR_CODES`, `isAiSettingsError(error)` and `openAssistantAiSettings({ env, loadModule })`, which loads Home through the existing `panel_module_loaders.ensureHomePanelModule()` and calls the existing `open_home_panel` — no second panel path and no second error list. `eVe/voice/assistant/assistant_runtime.js#open()` absorbs exactly those codes before `abortOpen` (state `phase: 'error'` + message, `connecting` → `appearing`, diagnostic `voice.assistant.open.ai_settings_required`, resolve instead of reject) and, while the dock owns no session and carries that error, makes the next activation the retry that reconnects instead of the early return that did nothing, `sendText()` resolves its failure snapshot, and the api's `openAiSettings()` reports `voice.assistant.open_ai_settings.failed` and resolves `null` because the BevyUI handler cannot await it; every other error keeps the previous rethrow. The dock reuses its own action row: `eVe/voice/assistant/assistant_conversation_view.js` adds `assistant_open_ai_settings` through the shared `button()` node when `isAiSettingsError` matches, inside the message area that already renders `snapshot.error`. The landing owner is the Home panel: `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_home_state.js#readHomeSectionRequest` reads `section` / `subsection(s)` / `revealNodeId`, `bevy_panel_home_sections.js#openSections` opens the section without toggling and still runs `syncSettingsSections` (the vault behind the AI keys loads), `bevy_panel_home_runtime.js#mountHomeCard` applies the request on the first frame and reveals `home_ai_keys_accordion` once the profile answered, and `eVe/intuition/tools/user_home_panel_runtime.js#openHomePanel` remounts an already-open Home panel when a section is asked for, keeping the latched re-activation otherwise. Evidence: `tests/eve/assistant_visual_contract.test.mjs` (17/17), `tests/eve/bevy_panel_home_contract.test.mjs` (landing test green; three pre-existing failures unchanged at HEAD).

2026-09-26 Assistant dock corner, history and accessories: `eVe/voice/assistant/assistant_dock.js#resolveAssistantDockGeometry({ width, height })` resolves one anchor — `x = width - dockWidth`, `iconX = dockWidth - size` — so the dock always opens in the bottom-right corner of the project surface, whichever gesture asked for it: the ribbon's Atome handle (`bevy_ui_main_menu_runtime.js`) and Mystic (`bevy_ui_mystic_runtime.js`) both invoke the same `window.eveAssistantApi`, so one geometry serves both. The width stays the shipped input row — one field plus the two accessory squares it carries — instead of the field alone, and `assistant_conversation_view.js` owns the left column the dock now shows: one shared `scroll_area` (`overflow: scroll_y`, panel control colour, existing scrollbar contract) carrying one text row per message (`assistant_history_turn_N`, `assistantConversationTurns`), revealed on the last message at `open()` and at each new last turn through the existing `revealTreeNode` with the dock edge inset, never re-revealed by an unrelated refresh. The conversation survives `close()`: the runtime keeps the turns, the dock only unmounts its tree. The input reuses `createRibbonInlineSearchRuntime` and `buildInlineSearchNodes` exactly like Communication, with two accessory squares from the same shared content owner: submit and microphone (icon `microphone` while listening, `mute` while not, `active` from the live session). The merged transcript was removed from `buildAssistantConversationNodes` so the history exists once. Evidence: `tests/eve/assistant_visual_contract.test.mjs`.

2026-09-26 Palette quick mode on both ribbons — one shared route, no new component: `buildBevyMenuToolNode` (`bevy_ui_menu_surface.js`) turns a press-opened palette parent's `activate` into `palette_open`, a hold-tool parent's `long_press` into `palette_slide_open` and each child's `activate` into `palette_choose`; `bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js` opens a press palette on the press itself and arms a slide one so the first movement past the stillness tolerance emits it — the tap keeps the tool's own action, the still hold keeps its delay, the glide opens and chooses without a lift — announces the option under the finger through the ordinary hover pair, and applies it on release before the menu closes (`setActivePalette('')` in the ribbon, `state.activePaletteKey = ''` in the rail). `resolveBevyToolPopupPositions` owns the glued geometry: the bottom ribbon stacks the options upward in the tool's own column (`bevy_ui_main_menu_model.js`, `orientation: 'horizontal'` with the anchor on the ribbon row), the lateral rail fans them out on the tool's row towards the centre of the screen (`atome_contextual_edit_model.js`, default orientation), both touching the tool with no gap. The unread `palette_drag_selection` field of `palette_choose` was deleted. Evidence: `tests/eve/contextual_rail_active_zone.test.mjs` (real rail model + real handlers + `normalizeBevyUiTree` + real pointer runtime: the tap keeps the tool's action, the horizontal slide opens without a lift, the release applies the option and closes), `tests/eve/bevy_ui_main_menu_contract.test.mjs` (R1 press-and-slide), `tests/eve/bevy_ui_main_menu_palette_motion.test.mjs`.

2026-09-26 Copy and paste stay centralized, and the Contact panel puts the main user first: `eVe/default/shortcuts.js` already registers meta/ctrl + c/x/v as the single shortcut table for `ui.copy.action`, `ui.cut.action` and `ui.paste.action`, and now carries a focused contract. `bevy_panel_contact_runtime.js#displayEntries` keeps the current user in the projected list and `selectable()` answers the pick gesture only for the other contacts, while `bevy_panel_contact_view.js` keeps the current user's row activatable — so the user reaches their own settings/themselves from the lateral rail instead of a dead first row. Evidence: `tests/eve/history_shortcuts.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_panel_contact_pick_contract.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/bevy_panel_contact_contract.test.mjs`.

The look is restored, not re-created: `eVe/elements/look/tool_theme.js` carries the lasso entry (background, border, `boxSizing: border-box`, radius, `box-shadow`) and `eVe/elements/look/eve_presets.js` re-publishes it as `evePresets.lasso.css`, so the project layer keeps one reader and one writer. 2026-09-25 Mystic release, dwell and fling: `eVe/intuition/ribbon/bevy_ui_mystic_runtime.js` now owns one stationary-dwell timer (`MYSTIC_AUTO_OPEN_DWELL_MS` = `RIBBON_TOKENS.mysticAutoOpenDwellMs`, 1500 ms then and 750 ms since 2026-09-28, `MYSTIC_AUTO_OPEN_DWELL_TOLERANCE_PX` 6 px, source `bevy_mystic_dwell`) for the four persistent tools, the palettes and the root return, in place of the deleted immediate hover opening; the release launches the tool OR opens the palette under the finger (`bevy_mystic_release`) but never the centre, never the root return and never a `disabled` item, and `MYSTIC_POST_REPLACEMENT_MOVE_PX` (12 px) of fresh movement is required after any geometry replacement; `activateDirectional({ direction, event, source })` resolves a cardinal fling to the configured cross slot (`bevy_mystic_fling`, root only, sources in `eVe/intuition/mystic/context.js`). 2026-09-25 Active-tool rail repair: `eVe/intuition/runtime/eve_intuition/atome_contextual_edit_projection_runtime.js` owns the extracted disposable rail projection, serialized rendering, activation order and existing shared pulse. The model resolves labels/icons; `eVe/intuition/tools/core/active_tool_registry.js` supplies live facts and invocation targets. Runtime and handlers consume the same displayed definitions. Recording bootstrap retains the originating Molecule group until stop. Evidence: `tests/eve/contextual_rail_active_target.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/contextual_rail_transport_stop.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/contextual_rail_recording_owner.test.mjs`.

2026-09-23 Terminal renderer failures paint their cause instead of ending on a blank page: `eVe/intuition/runtime/eve_intuition/boot_failure_surface.js` is the single owner of what a dead display lane can still say — plain DOM, no renderer, no design system, because the lane that would draw it is the one that just failed (the same reason `TAURI_LOCAL_HTTP_FAILURE_SCRIPT` exists in `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/lib.rs`). `boot_runtime.js` keeps the ordering it already owned: the new `settleTerminalWorkspaceBoot(result)` is the one path that stops the retries for good, and it now calls `presentBootFailureSurface({ failure: result })` after recording `workspaceBootOpenTerminalReason`. The surface is idempotent by id (`eve_boot_failure`), paints only the two terminal renderer patterns (`bevy_renderer_wasm_panic:`, `bevy_renderer_start_failed_terminal:`), names the cause it can prove (`gpu_unavailable`, `present_timeout`, `renderer_failed`), carries the exact failure message a support log needs, offers one Retry that reloads the page, and stays silent on `remote_account_not_provisioned` and on every transient failure the boot still retries — the login surface carries the action that fixes an unprovisioned account, so painting over it would hide the cure behind the symptom. i18n: `eve.boot.failure.*` (title, gpu_unavailable, present_timeout, renderer_failed, data_intact) in both locales. It also writes one self-report line in the device log (`[boot-failure] surface painted: <cause key> <message>`) because the app's diagnostics owners belong to the dead lane; `scripts/android/apk.sh` greps that prefix and prints a `boot failure surface painted` verdict next to its white-screen verdicts. Evidence: `tests/eve/boot_failure_surface_contract.test.mjs` (painted surface, exact detail text, idempotence, injected retry, plus two real boot-runtime runs: terminal paints and stops retrying, transient retries and paints nothing); the module sits inside the bundled critical graph (`chunks/eve/critical-bootstrap.js` of `temp/ios_runtime_package_verify`). The Android/emulator acceptance of the real run stays To verify.

2026-09-23 Dashboard filter hides the fixed modules: `dashboard_news_modules.js#items(source, { filteredCategoryId })` omits the weather (`news`) and clock (`calendar`) module cards whenever a filter is open, and `dashboard_bevy_ui_runtime.js#readItemsForRender` — its only caller — passes `state.filteredCategoryId`, so no foreign pinned card survives in the cascaded lanes. The module runtime is never stopped (minute timer, 15-minute weather refresh, loaded city and readings), so restoring the base display re-projects both cards immediately without a new weather read, and the memoized items map stays untouched. Evidence: `tests/probes/dashboard_weather_runtime.test.mjs`, `tests/eve/dashboard_bevy_ui_runtime_contract.test.mjs`, `temp/rail_dashboard_check.mjs` (browser probe, not executed here).

2026-09-22 Dashboard rail two-tier tools: every rail header splits into an upper two thirds band (tool icon + rubrique label, the filter surface) and a lower third band (`Nouveau`, the creation surface). `dashboard_layout.js#dashboardHeaderBands` is the single geometry owner: `createDashboardLayout({ filteredCategoryId })` exposes `header_filter_rect` / `header_new_rect` per lane and `hitTestDashboardLayout` keeps `kind: 'header'` while adding `zone: 'filter' | 'new'`; `dashboard_header_records.js` derives the icon/label bounds, the `2 px` `header_separator_*` line (inset by `tokens.metrics.gap`, `headerSeparator` token) and the `header_new_*` label from that same helper, so no constant is duplicated. `dashboard_environment.js#itemsForRender(categories, filteredCategoryId, itemsByCategory)` owns the cascade projection (the filtered items cascade from their own lane, every other lane is emptied) and its memo key includes the filter id. `dashboard_bevy_ui_runtime.js` owns `state.filteredCategoryId`, mutually exclusive with the creation `state.activeCategoryId`: the lower band keeps `activateCategory` unchanged, the upper band toggles the filter through `toggleCategoryFilter` with a targeted category reload and `scrollByLane = {}`, and no transition module is reintroduced — vertical and horizontal scrolling stay the layout's. i18n: `eve.dashboard.header.new` (`Nouveau` / `New`). Evidence: eVe/documentations/FRAMEWORK_STATE.md.

2026-09-22 Contact vignette live refresh: `eVe/domains/user/contact_change_events.js` is the shared subscription contract for the contact population — it re-exports `eve:people-directory-updated` (dispatched by `atome/src/squirrel/contacts/bootstrap.js` after a local contact create/update/delete) and `eve:user-profile-updated` (dispatched by `eVe/domains/user/profile_api.js` after a profile identity commit). `eVe/domains/dashboard/dashboard_data_controller.js` consumes it through `connectDirectoryChanges()` beside the existing `public_directory_events.js` subscription, both feeding one coalesced `refreshContactsPopulation()` closure that drops the cached `contacts` items (even while the Dashboard is closed, like `connectProjectChanges()`) and reloads plus repaints only while the Dashboard is active, so a renamed contact or a replaced photo repaints its Dashboard vignette without an application restart and a reopened Dashboard never repaints the stale cache. The invalidation was documented in the maps since the Bevy UI Dashboard migration (`cdf9b5e6`) but had no runtime subscriber, because `domains/dashboard/dashboard_data_invalidation.js` was deleted in that commit. Evidence: `tests/probes/contact_change_invalidation_contract.probe.mjs`, `tests/probes/public_directory_invalidation_contract.probe.mjs`, `eVe/documentations/FRAMEWORK_STATE.md`.

2026-09-22 Dashboard rail Settings removal: dashboard_layout.js reserves one rail header per visible category only — the extra settings slot, its index shift and the layout.settings geometry are gone, and hitTestDashboardLayout no longer answers a settings kind. dashboard_records.js paints no __eve_dashboard_header_bg_settings record and dashboard_svg_sources.js carries no settings icon. dashboard_bevy_ui_runtime.js registers no settings hit, and dashboard_actions.js exposes activateItemAction and openProjectItem only: openSettings, its openHomePanel injection and the internal home-module bridge are removed, so the main-toolbar Home tool is the single entry point of the Home panel. Evidence: eVe/documentations/FRAMEWORK_STATE.md.

The render runtime no longer recreates pointer sessions during painting; the unused BevyUI capturePointerSession method is removed.
