  selection. Closed lists retain data only and build no hierarchy subtree.
- `bevy_panel_info_drag_runtime.js`: row/handle drag session, canvas/UI rejection
  and the existing typed `ui.duplicate` invocation.
- `bevy_ui_{runtime,scroll_runtime}.js` and `bevy_panel_runtime.js`: internal
  `preserveNodeId` capture/compensation across a tree refresh.
- `tool_runtime_atome_mutation.js`: after the one duplicate `commitBatch`, reuse
  `eveToolBase.loadProjectAtomes(projectId, { force: true })` to project the new
  durable IDs into the existing shared scene. `realtime_atome_events_runtime.js`
  can also construct the complete set record from the canonical event while a
  state refresh is still pending.
- `bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js`: shared optional `long_press` ownership transfer;
  ordinary movement remains available to the scroll runtime before the hold.
- `bevy_panel_finder_{runtime,view}.js`: tools-scope hold/drag/release/cancel,
  lifetime of the existing active Tool Registry payload, and project-row
  delegation to the existing `project_data.activateProjectWorkspace` owner.
  Project rows never terminate at generic Atome selection or maintain a second
  current-project state.
- `atome_contextual_tool_drop_runtime.js`: contextual-tree hit validation,
  insertion index, reorder/deduplication and atomic ordered-list commit.

# Molecule v2 code map — 2026-08-12

- Canonical schema primitives: `eVe/intuition/tools/molecule/kernel/schemas.js`; complete snapshot validation: `kernel/timeline_validator.js`; clean v2 construction: `kernel/timeline_factory.js`.
- Structural reducers: `kernel/structure.js`; clip/crop/Record replacement reducers: `kernel/reducers.js`; frame/sample and musical projections: `kernel/time_model.js`.
- Durable routing and undo/batch: `session/session.js` and `session/timeline_operations.js`; persistence: `persistence/index.js`; runtime/API bridge: `runtime.js`, `eVe/intuition/tools/timeline_actions.js`, and `atome/src/squirrel/ai/default_tools_timeline.js`.
- Timeline view (BevyUI tree, 2026-10-14): `eVe/domains/rendering/project_view_timeline_content.js` (state, playhead, transport, rail; since 2026-10-15 also the project row's arrangement and scrub: `footerTimeline()`, `footerScrub()`, consumed by `project_view_surface_tree.js` and `project_view_surface_runtime.js#applyFooterOutcome`; `project_view_footer.js#transportMotionUpdates` moves the row's playhead), `_layout.js` (geometry, palette, ruler marks, snap), `_tree.js` (headers, lanes, clips, Record regions, prompteur line ticks, automation dots, detail, two-level ruler), `_gestures.js` (move/crop/loop/region/ruler/zoom/drop), `_rail.js` (Quantize, Zoom, Métronome, Input, track Volume/Delete), `_recording.js` (take on the fly), `_owner.js` (owner = project, seeded from the List plan). Since 2026-10-15: `_scenes.js` (Scenes band of the ruler: draw/move/resize), `_cells.js` + `_cell_gestures.js` + `_cells_rail.js` + `_cells_midi.js` (Cells beside the lanes, `view.layout` timeline|cells|split, Edit/Play, lasso, MIDI learn/launch), `_clip_rail.js` (block tools: split, join, trim, loop, stretch, fades, take, region takes), `_primitives.js` (shared node helpers).
- Mixer (2026-10-14): model `kernel/mix_modules.js` (module/effect catalogue), `mix_schema.js` (document, strip ids, migration), `mix_routing.js` (destinations, loops, pruning, transfers), `mix_projection.js` (strips/groups from content), `mix_structure.js` (`mix.*` reducers). Engine: `atome/engines/mix-graph` (Rust: `graph.rs`, `module.rs`, `effects.rs`, `dsp.rs`, `shared.rs`, `host.rs`), native `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/audio_engine/{mix,aux_outputs,live_input}.rs`, Web `platforms/web/audio-wasm/src/mix.rs`; JS `eVe/core/media_engine/molecule_mix_engine.js` (compiler, aggregation, graph capability), `molecule_mix_video.js`, `molecule_mix_live_web.js`; MIDI `eVe/intuition/tools/molecule/runtime_midi.js`. View: `eVe/domains/rendering/project_view_molecule_mix_content.js` (orchestrator) + `molecule_mix_{catalog,layout,strip_tree,module_controls,pickers,interactions,groups}.js`. Shared bricks: `bevy_panel_fader.js`, `bevy_panel_tile_gesture.js`, `bevy_panel_lasso.js`, horizontal touch scroll in `bevy_ui_scroll_runtime.js`.
- Molecule List projection reuses `project_view_list_content.js`, `bevy_panel_selectable_list.js`, and the extracted shared `bevy_panel_virtual_list.js` window math; main Create palette ownership remains `main_menu_content_runtime.js` plus the canonical registered Text, Draw, Code and Page tools.
- `project_view_molecule_info.js` projects Molecule/Section/Track properties into the existing Info runtime and enters the existing contextual rail in rail-only mode. Its `playMoleculeContextualOwner` keeps Ensemble on the native Molecule transport and forwards sequential/random rules to `project_view_playback_runtime.js`; its Delete item first lazy-loads the existing Delete tool owner, then routes the selected canonical owner to `ui.delete.selection`. `deleteCanonicalMolecule` commits owner/direct-member deletion and closes the registered timeline API after success so timeline, transport, and scene projections are disposed. `atome_contextual_edit_runtime.js` owns the generic disposable virtual-target seam; neither the rail nor the view owns a delete mutation.
- `eVe/domains/media/shared/media_playback_command.js` owns the immutable internal playback command used at every media boundary: canonical source identity, normalized playback kind, timeline start, natural or explicitly edited duration, source-in, rate, gain, pan, mute and visual order. Persisted `video_recording`/`video` and `audio_recording`/`audio`/`sound` values are normalized only here and are never rewritten in storage.
- `eVe/domains/media/shared/media_playback_audio_executor.js` is the single Kira execution owner shared by standalone, Ensemble and Molecule adapters. It owns unlock, source/extracted-video-audio preparation, asset load, voice start/stop and cleanup; `molecule.native.js` now delegates its PlayRecordCore adapter to this executor instead of maintaining a second implementation. Load payload composition applies the explicit occurrence `assetId` after source or facade payload fields, so normalized empty source ids cannot redirect registration to the media URL. Prepared Molecule assets are reused on resume/seek/scrub; the forced reload and missing-clip voice retry are removed.
- `runtime_transport.js` owns only scoped Molecule scheduling and the ephemeral ordered List sequence projection. It compiles persisted Sections/Tracks into shared media commands, preserves explicit crops/rates/gains/pans, applies no implicit stretch, and lets the session end at the latest command. `runtime.js` coordinates existing Record schedulers at the returned List offsets; `timeline_actions.js` owns the UI action registrations.
- `molecule_session_clips.js` prepares shared Kira assets for audio and extracted video audio only. Molecule visuals remain exclusively on the muted Bevy decoder; no hidden video, metadata-duration override or parallel raster pool exists in the session.
- Removed code: `retired:eVe/intuition/tools/molecule/panel/index.js`, `footer_tools_contract.js`, and `migrateMoleculeTimelineSnapshot`.
- Focused coverage: `tests/probes/molecule_v2_structure.probe.mjs` plus the maintained `npm run test:molecule` manifest.
- Exhaustive acceptance: `tests/probes/molecule_eve_ui_acceptance_probe.mjs` owns the complete real Web journey and its optional audio/video Record/playback endurance; `molecule_ui_acceptance_support.mjs` owns canvas-to-client hit geometry plus real mouse/touch move, crop, lasso, zoom and split routing. `molecule_ui_drop_acceptance_scenarios.mjs` is the thin three-view entry point, `molecule_ui_drop_core.mjs` owns fixture/drop/reload assertions, `molecule_ui_drop_playback_support.mjs` owns shared hierarchy and playback observation, and the `molecule_ui_drop_{natural,list,matrix}.mjs` modules own only their view-specific gestures. Each drop journey uses real pointer input, reloads canonical projection, checks `parent_id`, playback progression/natural end and the absence of floating members. `scripts/run_molecule_ui_{matrix,repeat}.mjs` compose deterministic visual and repetition campaigns; `tests/performance/molecule_acceptance_performance.mjs` owns the 30-run kernel/virtualization budgets.
- `scripts/run_molecule_acceptance_repeat.mjs` runs the complete acceptance three times without retry and writes one machine-readable campaign report while assigning distinct visual and endurance evidence tags to every pass.
- Platform acceptance: `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/local_http_navigation.rs` owns bounded local-listener readiness for the sole runtime in `lib.rs`; `main.rs` only calls `squirrel_lib::run()` and must not duplicate bootstrap, server, command, or plugin registration. `build:molecule:tauri` builds the current debug `.app`; `build:molecule:ios` builds the app and AUv3 extension for the Simulator; and `build:molecule:ios:device` signs the generic iPhoneOS app plus embedded AUv3 extension without claiming physical-device interaction.

## iOS waveform and project Record ownership — 2026-08-21

- `atome/src/application/audio_runtime/record_audio_api.js` normalizes native terminal `waveform_peaks` (with `peaks` as a compatibility alias) to finite absolute values in `[0, 1]`, bounded at 256 entries, before media persistence.
- `AppNativeAudioRecording.swift` and `AUv3RecorderAnalysis.swift` are native post-stop waveform owners. AUv3 analyses the closed WAV outside its realtime render callback; `AUv3Recorder.swift` sends both contract names in `record_done`.
- `media_persistence_service.js` persists the canonical peak arrays on the audio-recording Atome. `project_audio_waveform_renderer.js`, `render_atom.js`, and the existing Bevy media resolver project those durable arrays; playback progress remains a separate WebGPU style overlay.
- `project_view_capture_to_timeline.js` requests one Molecule batch for Record replacement. `runtime.js` and `molecule_stores.js` commit timeline snapshot plus owner `playback_mode` together through `Atome.commitBatch`; the surface rereads the owner before treating Stop as successful. `playback_rules.js` rejects a performance marker with no usable clips explicitly, and `clearGroupTimelinePerformance`/`ui.timeline.performance.delete` clear clips and restore sequential mode through the same batch boundary.

## Structured Molecule playback and selection — 2026-08-21

- `tool_runtime_atome_mutation.js` builds the schema-v2 timeline from direct `parent_id` members in the same canonical creation, absorb, and merge batch. Each member occupies one content track at zero; the existing Molecule transport remains the only player. `backfillCanonicalMoleculeTimeline` repairs an older owner exactly once before it is played.
- `project_view_playback_runtime.js` owns queue-preserving manual jumps. List and Matrix only emit intent: a first inactive tap selects, a second tap clears selection, and an active queue jump restarts the chosen item while retaining the already-frozen queue order.
- `project_view_contextual_rail.js` marks its disposable structured rail record. The active `getAtomeContextualEditApi().enter(...)` bridge in `boot_runtime.js` forwards that record to `atome_contextual_rail_runtime.js`; the rail resolver and invocation runtime preserve the marker through the persistent Bevy contextual rail, expose Play for every selected List/Matrix item kind, and delegate it to `project_view_playback_runtime.js`. That runtime owns `isPlayingTarget(...)` for structured item, container, queue, and adopted-Molecule Play/Stop presentation; the rail model and `project_view_surface_context_runtime.js` consume it rather than reading selected-media state directly. The context runtime force-refreshes the current rail target on each playback-state announcement; it reopens the container rail only when no row/cell target exists and never lets `playingIds` own selection. Neither Mystic nor an inline List/Matrix control owns this behavior.
- In that structured rail only, `atome_contextual_rail_model_runtime.js` resolves the parent `record_action` presentation through the existing `record_action_live` behavior. The visible item remains the single Record action toggle with `{ mode: "live" }`; it never expands the key/live/audio/video capture palette. Natural contextual editing keeps its existing type-specific Record presentation.
- `atome_contextual_edit_{runtime,model}.js`: transient preview index and Bevy
  rail spacing. `atome_edit_footer_{runtime,model_runtime}.js` reloads and
  persists the established `footer_tools` list.
- Persistent evidence: `bevy_panel_info_contract.test.mjs`, pointer/Contact/touch contracts
  `bevy_ui_layout_contract.test.mjs`, `project_scene_gesture_performance.test.mjs`
  and the Vitest manifest guard.

# Workspace presentation ownership — 2026-08-18

- `eVe/domains/rendering/surface_runtime.js` is the only workspace-size observer and publishes distinct stabilized sizes through `subscribeRenderSurfaceSize`. Dashboard, main menu and structured project views subscribe to it and must not add viewport or surface resize observers of their own.
- `bevy_ui_render_queue_runtime.js` owns per-tree latest-wins generations and abort signals; image hydration and overlay reconciliation consume that signal. `bevy_ui_unmount_runtime.js` owns final disposal. Product runtimes may cancel a stale tree through `cancelTreeRender` but may not create another queue.
- `project_view_mode_state.js` owns latest user intent without per-project FIFO and applies Natural through the same canonical surface transition as List/Matrix, so an older structured tree cannot survive a project restore. It records explicit intent before asynchronous surface work, checks the intent sequence again after persistence reads, and commits same-mode choices because the local-first in-memory default can differ from durable state. `project_view_records.js` owns canonical paged project reads, preserves `meta.project_id` membership, and excludes canonical deletion tombstones; `project_view_reorder_runtime.js` owns durable `hierarchy_order` writes and uses one canonical batch when available. The canonical shared drag session owns pointer preservation and drag cleanup; List renders a source fade plus insertion gap, while Matrix consumes the same press/drag/release lifecycle. `project_view_surface_runtime.js` retains a pending pointer-node preservation request across queued renders. List and Matrix rebuild their selected projection from canonical selection on load. `project_view_surface_context_runtime.js` owns the contextual rail and `project_view_surface_events.js` owns canonical change/size subscriptions.
- `project_data.js` owns a single global latest-wins workspace-activation generation. It starts the persisted-mode prefetch through `project_view_mode_state.js`, consumes the authoritative `loadProjectAtomes` projection, then calls that same owner's guarded restoration once after loading. The activation load carries internal `viewModePrepared: true`; `tool_genesis_project_load_runtime.js` skips only its redundant restoration in that case. A Natural/Matrix/List gesture after prefetch invalidates the old result and same-mode Natural is still committed, so a late restore cannot overwrite the user. Boot loads await the same single restoration before publishing project readiness; the authoritative refresh never starts a second one.
- `dashboard_bevy_ui_runtime.js` owns complete-data-before-presentation; `dashboard_presentation_runtime.js` owns the cancellable GPU fade and `dashboard_bevy_ui_diagnostics.js` owns readiness diagnostics. Closing cancels pending tree work before suspension.

# Canonical List hierarchy and recursive transport — 2026-08-27

- `project_view_visual_panel.js` disables waveform progress through the existing record/composite-preview option. `virtual_scene_contract.js` and `project_scene_invalidation_runtime.js` honor that projection-only visibility flag; row/footer progress and video decoder identity are preserved. `bevy_ui_tree_motion_runtime.js` converts local-position updates to screen-space deltas, preventing a footer head at local y=0 from escaping to the top of the canvas.
- `project_view_navigation.js`, `project_view_molecule_list_model.js`, and `project_view_list_content.js` project only canonical Atome and Molecule records. `parent_id` owns membership and `hierarchy_order` owns sibling order; legacy Timeline Sections, Tracks, and Clips never manufacture List rows.
- `tool_runtime_list_model_migration.js` converts legacy editable Timeline snapshots through one idempotent `Atome.commitBatch`. `project_view_list_migration_runtime.js` invokes it before List projection and reloads canonical state after a successful transaction.
- `project_view_transport_plan.js` compiles nested sequential, simultaneous, random, and performance branches. `project_view_transport_runtime.js` owns the sole bounded clock, explicit transport commands, global/local time mapping, mute gates, and ephemeral progress snapshots.
- Recursive snapshots carry `rootRecord` and `activeLeafRecords`. `project_view_visual_subject.js` consumes those leaves independently of row selection/expansion; hierarchy path ids remain row-state signals, not media subjects. Surface events synchronize Visual before the transport resolves video nodes and discard a stale level snapshot when object playback takes over.
- `bevy_video_decode_source_runtime.js` retains the latest active/paused position in its existing per-window request registry, including requests preceding projection. Continuous playback tolerates sub-250 ms drift instead of seeking every tick; explicit seek/scrub remains precise and `seeked` redraws paused frames. It reuses `bevy_projection_media_contract.js` timeline normalization; no parallel decoder or timer was added.
- `project_view_footer.js` projects the global playhead. `project_view_list_view.js` and the shared `bevy_panel_selectable_list*` modules project fixed 44 px Atome/Molecule rows and patch only visible active local playheads.
- `project_view_list_preview_{model,gesture}.js`, `bevy_panel_record_timeline_preview.js`, and `project_view_occurrence_edit_runtime.js` own derived previews, scrub, crop, move, split, duplicate, join, and Rubber Band stretch orchestration. Media data and `parent_id` remain canonical; generated thumbnails and stretch assets are transient.
- `project_view_drop_intent_runtime.js` owns the five drop zones. `tool_runtime_molecule_combine.js` preserves Molecule envelopes, inserts directly into a matching mode, or creates a canonical wrapper through the existing mutation facade.
- `project_view_surface_runtime.js` remains the sole structured-view orchestrator; tree composition and unmount cleanup are focused internal modules, not parallel routes.

# Public directory implementation — 2026-08-30

- Server projection and invalidation: `server/directoryPublicService.js`, `server/wsAtomeOperations.js`; schema compatibility: `database/adole_schema_migrations.js` and `database/schema.sql`.
- Client population owners: `eVe/intuition/tools/contact_model.js`, `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_finder_data.js`, and `eVe/intuition/tools/communication_users.js`; Dashboard reuses Contact projection through `eVe/domains/dashboard/dashboard_data_adapters.js`.
- Shared missing-photo projection: `atome/src/squirrel/components/media_card_contract.js` resolves canonical `assets/images/icons/user.svg`; Bevy Contact and Dashboard consumers only project it and never persist it.

# Shared Home/current-Contact profile implementation — 2026-08-30

- `eVe/domains/user/profile_api.js` no longer exposes an identity-only mutation (2026-09-22): the current user's card is edited only in Home.
- `bevy_panel_contact_runtime.js` unfolds the current user's row with the Home card itself (`mountHomeCard` / `buildHomeCard` / `handleHomeCardEvent` from `bevy_panel_home_runtime.js`): same state, same sections, Réglages and session action included.
- `bevy_panel_home_state.js` contains the Home panel's state construction and read projection extracted from the runtime; it introduces no persistence path.
# Directory identity and Login reentry repair — 2026-08-30

- `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/server/local_atome_sync_worker.rs`: conditional local-profile `atome_id` to remote-principal normalization.
- `platforms/desktop-tauri/src/server/local_atome.rs` and `local_atome_extended.rs`: current-profile bootstrap enqueue after remote sync configuration.
- `server/directoryPublicService.js`: principal-vault profile source, restart rebuild, unchanged-profile revision suppression, requester exclusion, redacted photo projection, and the single-principal `refreshPrincipal` used by `server/wsPhoneLinkAuth.js` after `update-user`, after account deletion and after any auth action that emits a token, so the projection and its `directory.public` event follow an account change immediately.
- `eVe/domains/user/public_directory_events.js`: shared remote-directory invalidation subscription used by Contact, Dashboard, Finder, and Communication. `eVe/domains/user/contact_change_events.js`: shared local contact population subscription used by the Dashboard Contacts category.


### 2026-09-07 — Dashboard cancellation and recursive playback input

`dashboard_lifecycle_runtime.js` limits stale-open cleanup to its own generation;
a superseded operation cannot suspend a newer tree. The Dashboard runtime checks
cancellation after first reveal. Workspace opening propagates cancelled results
before projection validation and bootstrap-ready publication.

`project_view_surface_context_runtime.js` reads the playback root once, shares
that record with rule resolution, and includes its canonical properties/timeline
in the recursive transport input even when visible rows exclude the project.
It removes the empty synthetic root and does not replace invalid Performance mode
with a sequential rail icon. State persistence and WebGPU ownership are unchanged.

### Structured presentation campaign — 2026-09-08

Structured presentation: project_view_surface_layout owns shared tool-size/row geometry and bounded split heights; project_view_visual_fullscreen_runtime owns project/mode split persistence and session accordion state. project_view_surface_events owns transport snapshot projection. Fixed list rows and project footer reuse fixedHierarchyListRowGeometry.

### Structured selection transport convergence — 2026-09-08

project_view_surface_context_runtime.playSelection owns both single and multiple structured Play. project_view_playback_follow retargets that same transport; the footer no longer starts a second item clock. project_view_contextual_rail owns the shared project/Molecule Play Mode palette, with canonical playback_rules writes and immediate icon refresh. The recursive compiler skips full Mute only in sequential/random parents; simultaneous silence retains its timeline duration. Web selection_recursive_v2 and single_mix_recursive_v9 validate these paths.

### Live structured draft projection — 2026-09-08

project_view_create_draft_runtime extends the existing visual-changed event with record/project_id. project_view_surface_events updates the content projection before rendering, preventing its older record from replacing the current drawn SVG. Draw drafts use #ffffff00 until canonical geometry sets neutral #ffffff; SVG stroke paint owns brush color. Web draw_live_projection_fixed_v5 verifies held-pointer live pixels in List/Matrix.

### Text style focus contract — 2026-09-08

The existing text_editing_session accepts retainOnBlur (default false); text_bridge enables it for scene objects. Losing keyboard focus to a style tool stops the caret but retains the active projected range. Tab/explicit commit ends the scene edit. project_scene_text_edit_state invalidates ranges by lifecycle and collapse rather than a timer. Web partial_text_color_commit_v3 verifies partial color in both structured viewers and persistence after commit.

### Interaction campaign convergence — 2026-09-08

List/Matrix controllers apply canonical selection before project_view_playback_follow receives selectionIds; an empty set stops the recursive transport. svg_draw_runtime routes inactive-brush opacity to applySelectionStyleMutation; the draw size keeps the next-stroke brush and also restrokes every selected drawing through the restrokeDrawMarkup owner of the draw model, which moves the viewBox padding with the stroke so a thicker trace is never clipped — drawing cannot be disturbed by that, since an armed stroke is never left selected. project_view_list_view retains the shared virtualizer's computed window and uses it for transport updates, preventing bottom rows from disappearing after scrub. No new state authority or renderer.

The real-pointer harness remains molecule_eve_ui_acceptance_probe; molecule_ui_acceptance_support owns input geometry, molecule_ui_image_evidence owns pixel comparisons, molecule_ui_list_acceptance owns the List journey, molecule_ui_timeline_acceptance and molecule_ui_timeline_gestures own Timeline checks, and molecule_ui_media_fixture/hierarchy_gestures/media_playback_evidence own the respective layered-media responsibilities. Existing top-level entrypoints are retained.

Contextual interaction ownership (2026-09-08): `atome_contextual_edit_registry.js` owns the lazy public facade and scene intent installation; `atome_contextual_rail_runtime.js` resolves tool definitions and forwards them to the shared BevyUI contextual runtime. The six unused DOM row/drag/palette/slider/state/child modules were removed after consumer checks. Interaction labels live in `languages_{en,fr}_interaction.js`, assembled by `languages.js`.

Structural history (2026-09-08): database/adole_event_mutation.js captures identity preimages, server/atomeHistoryCommands.js constructs undo/redo events, and server/userVaultRouter.js plus server/userVaultProcess.js execute in the owner's vault. tests/server/user_vault_provider.probe.mjs covers real gateway routing, cross-principal refusal and Molecule creation/reparent undo/redo. project_view_matrix_content.js uses the full-project playback loader; bevy_panel_media_card.js delegates Molecule cards to the shared composite preview owner.

Choice palettes (2026-09-24, superseding the 2026-09-18 choice-icon contract): tool_definition_ssot.js owns resolveToolActiveChild alone (resolveToolChoiceChild, resolveToolChoiceIcon and entryForKey are removed); shared/tool_presentation.js resolves the label and the icon of the definition itself (explicit icon, then def.icon, then the key) and takes no children/activeToolIds; toolbox_runtime_model.buildMainEntries(content) and bevy_ui_main_menu_model.buildBevyMainMenuItems(content, ...) consume it, and atome_contextual_edit_model.js passes no activeToolIds. Both renderers still close on a leaf choice and replace a palette slot with its locked child (bevy_ui_main_menu_model.activeToolIdsFromLatchedState, atome_contextual_edit_runtime.activeToolIds); the keep_open_on_child_click / keepPaletteOpenOnChildClick opt-outs are gone. main_menu_content_runtime.js and main_menu_create_content_runtime.js no longer publish selectedChildKey for view/mode/record_action/page_create, bevy_ui_main_menu_runtime.js owns no PROJECT_VIEW_MODE_CHANGED_EVENT listener, and domains/rendering/project_view_contextual_rail.js marks the playback mode in force on the child instead of the slot icon. contextual_selection_levels.test.mjs, bevy_ui_main_menu_contract.test.mjs and create_tools_modes_contract.test.mjs cover slot identity, closure and slot replacement.

Lit tools (2026-09-24): the tools that stay lit once started now have one owner, `eVe/intuition/tools/core/active_tool_registry.js`, which declares each family's residence (`rail` for the record/playback family of the contextual rail, `menu` for text, drawing, code, page, placeholder and template) and the invocation path used both to read and to stop them; `tool_runtime_circle_tool_state.stopOperativeCreationTools` delegates to `stopActiveToolEntries`, and the table it duplicated is deleted. The lit presentation stays on the slot that displays the tool (the rail's Play becomes Stop; the ribbon keeps `shared/tool_presentation.js`), so nothing is duplicated in the registry. In the rail, `buildAtomeContextualEditTree({ activeSlots })` removes the lit rail tools from the ordinary level and pins one case per tool against `layout.bottom`, above the Atome handle, outside the scrolling viewport, pushing the ordinary stack up by exactly the reserved height; the pinned case keeps the level node id `atome_contextual_tool_<key>` (`…_icon`), so handler, tool identity and pulse are shared, and `atome_contextual_edit_runtime.js` keeps the activation order with the catalogue order as the cold-render fallback. A menu-resident lit tool keeps its ribbon slot and never gets a rail case. Both surfaces synchronize the unique `createRecordingIndicatorPulse` (`litToolIconIds()` for the rail, `resolveBevyMainMenuLitToolIconIds` through `litToolPulse` for the ribbon) and clear it at stop; a view mode that paints itself active never pulses. (2026-10-09: replaced by `createLitToolGlowPulse` — see ARCHITECTURE_MAP `### 2026-10-09 — Engaged tools glow`.) Covered by `tests/eve/contextual_rail_active_zone.test.mjs` plus `contextual_rail_scroll` and `bevy_ui_main_menu_contract`; real-runtime pixel acceptance in Web 3001 / Tauri remains to verify. The options a pinned tool opens on a long press stay anchored to that case and carry its palette accent when open; the case reads its own face from the level definition when the level still publishes it.

### 2026-09-09 interaction lifecycle checkpoint

- `project_view_transport_plan.js`: recursive plan plus pure engine-timeline projection (moved from runtime; existing public export retained by the runtime).
- `project_view_transport_runtime.js`: cancels superseded media preparation and releases foreign plans/resources through `releaseOutsideProject`.
- `project_workspace_activation_runtime.js`: awaits transport project-scope release before activating the next project.
- `bevy_ui_tree_motion_runtime.js`: inherited parent translation for flat projected children; structural local coordinates unchanged.

### Home profile read latency (2026-09-11)

Home keeps section headers mounted during pending/failed profile reads, with retry through its existing lifecycle and no editable default profile while unavailable. `loadHomeProfile` calls `loadUserProfile(null, { repair: false })`: the configured profile backend remains authoritative; optional legacy repair and remote photo retrieval do not gate panel reads. Other profile callers retain explicit/default migration behavior. Regression owners: `tests/eve/bevy_panel_home_contract.test.mjs` and `bevy_panel_home_lifecycle.test.mjs`. Physical iOS acceptance remains in progress.

OpenAI credential migration (2026-09-11): `provider_client.resolveOpenAiCredentialStatus` is the shared Home/assistant/catalog owner for migrating the authenticated account's legacy local token into the server vault. Existing server credentials are authoritative; successful storage precedes local removal. `provider_broker` enforces the expected principal before dispatch and on retries. No server secret is returned to clients. Regression evidence: `tests/eve/openai_credential_migration.test.mjs`.

### OpenAI editor completion and credential status (2026-10-10)

`eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_home_runtime.js` routes native blur, Enter and Bevy blur through the shared editor's completion callback to the existing `home.key.save`/`handleHomeVaultEvent` owner for AI drafts. `accessRuntime.commitField` remains the owner for ordinary profile fields; it intentionally excludes security fields. Validation and encrypted storage remain in `wsAiProviderOperations`/`providerCredentialVault`. `atome/src/squirrel/ai/provider_client.js` and `assistant_provider_config.js` only classify an explicit `configured: false` as an absent key; malformed status preserves legacy material and becomes `provider_credential_status_invalid`, with EN/FR interaction translations. Regression owners: `tests/eve/openai_key_editing.test.mjs`, `openai_key_configuration.test.mjs` and `assistant_routing.test.mjs`. Shared tests and real isolated OpenAI authentication pass. Physical iPhone/iOS 27.2 acceptance also receives real assistant answers with the existing key before and after full process relaunch, without key entry. Web/macOS UI acceptance remains open; initial portrait did not show history until rotation (the settled return to portrait did), and one initial WebContent termination recovered through native Retry (causes To verify).

### iOS event journal latency and atomic projection (2026-09-11)

`platforms/ios/atome-auv3/Common/AiSRuntimeEvents.swift` owns event normalization, journal append and projection as an extension of the existing AiSRuntime, extracted from LocalHTTPServer. Each event uses a SQLite savepoint so journal, particles and state-current commit or roll back together; enclosing remote-sync transactions retain ownership. Sequential batches retain per-event semantics. Sequence allocation includes `sequence IS NOT NULL`, matching the existing partial stream/sequence index rather than scanning the journal. The executable Swift probe covers rollback, duplicate IDs, nested transactions and index selection. Physical iPhone startup: 453 events / 1379 ms cumulative handler time, p50 3 ms, p95 4 ms, zero observed local request timeouts; assistant network and Mystic acceptance remain open.

`platforms/ios/atome-auv3/Common/AiSRuntimeStateCurrent.swift` owns the state-current read boundary as the same kind of AiSRuntime extension, extracted from LocalHTTPServer: message handling, `get`/`list`/`count`, row loading and serialization, `canReadState` authorization, upserts and Atome-meta lookup. It also owns the single read-only `capabilities` projection, applied only in `handleStateCurrentGet` and `handleStateCurrentList` with the same shape as Axum `project_capabilities_for_read` and Fastify `projectAtomeCapabilitiesForRead`; the shared serializer and snapshot creation never carry capabilities, so eVe `context_menu_resolver.js` keeps its menu-access gate on iOS without adding a second server, state owner or query. Regression owner: `tests/probes/ios_state_current_capabilities.probe.mjs`.

### Mystic layer band, iOS provider relay and voice bridge (2026-09-11)

`bevy_ui_mystic_model.js` owns the Mystic's absolute stacking: the renderer puts every node on one flat scale (`GlobalZIndex(style.z_index ?? 0)`), so the tile grid carries `workspaceSceneLayerOrder('mystic')` down its whole subtree, the way `bevy_panel_tree.js` carries its own. A node left without a `z_index` is painted at 0, underneath every panel. `bevy_ui_mystic_runtime.js` owns dismissal on the native surface, where the document-level owner never sees the press: the presentation root closes on a press outside the tile grid and ignores only the reprojection of the still-held opening pointer (not released, and within `RIBBON_TOKENS.mysticOriginPressTolerancePx` of the opening point). Regression owner: `tests/eve/bevy_ui_mystic_contract.probe.mjs`.

`FastifySyncClient.reloadConfiguration` (iOS) is idempotent: the application re-publishes the same remote configuration before every authenticated request, and disconnecting on each one closed the provider channels carrying the in-flight assistant request (`provider_connection_closed`). Only a changed configuration disconnects.

`atome/src/squirrel/voice/bootstrap.js` loads its bridge modules as `{ id, path }` descriptors through `loadModulesSequentially`, so the packaged runtime resolves them by id from `__ATOME_PACKAGED_MODULES__`. The packaged bundle has no file at those paths; importing them directly failed the whole voice bridge on iOS. Regression owners: `tests/eve/voice_playback_bootstrap.test.mjs`, `tests/atome/src/squirrel/voice/bootstrap.probe.mjs`.

### Panel input sessions under the Mystic menu (2026-09-11)

`text_editing_session.js` owns whether a blur ends a session: `retainOnBlur` (stay
active, look unfocused — scene text) and `reclaimFocusOnBlur` (take the DOM focus back
— an input box with its own menu over it) are values or predicates resolved at blur
time. `bevy_panel_text_editing.js` holds its session while the Mystic it opened really
stands — the recorded field and `isMysticMenuOpen()` at blur time — released by that
menu's `onClose` or by the first blur after it is gone, including a refused opening, and
its `paste()` replaces the whole value.
`bevy_ui_pointer_runtime.js` refuses the focus to a press landing on the `mystic` layer,
which the hit result now carries from `bevy_ui_runtime.js`. `bevy_ui_mystic_runtime.js`
owns its geometry across surface changes through `subscribeRenderSurfaceSize`,
re-deriving the centre from the stored opening point, and never interrupts a close.
Root cause, evidence and rejected hypotheses:
`known-bug-solutions/ios-panel-input-fast-tap-blur/README.md`.

### 2026-09-16 — Dashboard plein écran (état courant)

This section supersedes earlier Dashboard descriptions. `dashboard_layout.js` owns `surface_rect`, the five fixed top-to-bottom Calendar/News/Contacts/Monitor/Projects lanes, bottom alignment with Projects flush to the viewport, exact `1U × 1U` cards, the `2U × 1U` weather card, handedness mirroring, and the four-column mobile cap. It reserves neither menu height nor a creation column. `dashboard_records.js` orchestrates only the opaque profile base plus `cover` image, then delegates to `dashboard_header_records.js`, `dashboard_card_records.js`, and `dashboard_weather_card_records.js`. `dashboard_glass_material.js` derives supports from the shared `EVE_COMMON_SKIN_TOKENS.bevy.systemSurface` material. Every content cell, with or without media, first receives that support; `dashboard_skin.js#contentGlass` owns only its transparent fill, tint alpha, and blur radius, while `dashboard_skin.js#labelText` owns the fixed `16 px` ordinary-label size and the display-only eight-grapheme-plus-`.` abbreviation for longer values. `dashboard_card_records.js` then places project/contact media at opacity `1` without inheriting support transparency.

`workspace_main_menu_visibility.js` possède la suspension/réactivation du menu et démonte son tree pendant le Dashboard. `intuition_liquid_menu_renderer.js` empêche sa surface liquide de remonter pendant cette suspension et réconcilie aussi les projections orphelines. `user_workspace_surface_runtime.js` ferme et attend tous les panneaux enregistrés via `panel_globals.js`, puis suspend le menu avant la projection Dashboard ; `project_workspace_activation_runtime.js` le réactive après activation effective. `dashboard_news_modules.js` possède l’état météo jetable et, tant qu’un filtre est ouvert, `items` omet les deux modules — ils reviennent à l’affichage de base avec leurs relevés déjà chargés ; `dashboard_weather_preferences.js` persiste seulement `preferences.dashboard.weather_location` par l’API profil ; le Finder possède le géocodage inverse. Le rail est un outil à deux étages : les deux tiers hauts portent l’icône et le libellé de la rubrique et filtrent la catégorie (cascade des items depuis sa propre rangée, second appui pour revenir à l’affichage de base), le tiers bas porte le libellé `eve.dashboard.header.new` et garde la création ; la découpe appartient à `dashboard_layout.js#dashboardHeaderBands` et la ligne de séparation au token `headerSeparator`, sans moteur de défilement ni transition de focus supplémentaire. Le focus d’en-tête reste visuel seulement. L’ancien contrôleur de transition de focus, les records « + », les bandes, le voile et les ombres Dashboard ont été supprimés.

# Lit tools: pinned rail zone and single pulse — 2026-09-24

- `eVe/intuition/tools/core/active_tool_registry.js` (new) owns which tools stay lit and where they live: `ACTIVE_TOOL_SURFACE` (`rail` | `menu`), `ACTIVE_TOOL_ENTRIES`, `isActiveToolId(toolId)` (a lit tool, not an open panel or palette), `isActiveRailToolKey(key)`, `readActiveToolEntries({ keepToolId })` and `stopActiveToolEntries({ keepToolId })`. It reuses the tool's own invocation path (`deactivate`, `close`, `setPageToolActive`, `setActive`) plus the published latch release, so no second command system exists; the lit label and icon stay on the slot that displays the tool.
- `atome_contextual_edit_model.js` owns the rail's pinned zone: `withoutMovedTools` removes the moved roots from the ordinary level, `pinnedHeight = min(n × itemSize, availableRailHeight)` is reserved against `layout.bottom`, the scrolling viewport keeps `availableRailHeight - pinnedHeight`, and `contextualToolNode` builds both a level case and a pinned case from the same definition and the same node id.
- `atome_contextual_edit_runtime.js` owns the activation order (`state.activeSlotOrder`, catalogue-order fallback) and returns the lit rail cases through `activeSlots()`; `litToolIconIds()` feeds the unique `createRecordingIndicatorPulse`. (2026-10-09: replaced by `createLitToolGlowPulse` — see ARCHITECTURE_MAP `### 2026-10-09 — Engaged tools glow`.)
- `bevy_ui_main_menu_model.resolveBevyMainMenuLitToolIconIds({ items, latchedByToolId, isStayLitTool })` returns the ribbon slots that display a lit tool (an expandable slot reads its locked `activeChild`, never an open panel); `bevy_ui_main_menu_runtime.js` owns the matching `litToolPulse` and clears it on unmount.
- Tests: `tests/eve/contextual_rail_active_zone.test.mjs` (pinned geometry and handedness, non-scrolling zone, non-duplication, no rail case for a menu-resident tool, stable ids, pulse sync and clear) with `contextual_rail_scroll` and `bevy_ui_main_menu_contract`; `npm run check:syntax` stays green. Real-runtime pixel acceptance in Web 3001 / Tauri remains to verify.

# Pinned rail case without a rail, and the stop no selection may block — 2026-09-24

- `active_tool_registry.js` gains `readActiveRailToolEntries()`: the lit facts of the rail family that run with no level (record through `record_action_state.js` or the capture owner's `BOOTSTRAP_RECORD_ACTION_RUNTIME_STATE`; playback through `selected_project_media_playback_state.js` then `project_view_transport_runtime.js`), each with the `extraInput` its stop needs.
- `atome_contextual_edit_runtime.js` reads those slots BEFORE its early returns and owns `mountPinnedZoneOnly()`: `definitions: []`, `activeAtomeId: ''`, a `…_pinned_shadow` node and the same `recordingPulse.sync(litToolIconIds())`. `install()` listens to the record/playback/tool-state events, so the zone repaints with the rail closed.
- `atome_contextual_edit_handlers.js` answers a pinned-only case (`extraDefinitions`) through `runActiveDefinition`, and turns a lit rail palette off when its level publishes no locked child.
- `atome_contextual_rail_definition_invocation_runtime.js` passes the definition's latched state to the selection guard (`active: definition?.active === true`), so a stop is never reported as `no_selection_target`; `atome_contextual_rail_runtime.js` drops its now-duplicate playback listener.
- Tests: `contextual_rail_active_zone.test.mjs` 16/16 covers the rail-less record and playback cases, the live stop payload and the object change; the adjacent rail/menu suites stay green. Real-runtime pixel acceptance remains to verify.

# Selection rail: one anchored placement for Copie and Contact — 2026-09-26

- The selection case of a list was already the canonical component: `bevy_panel_choice.js` composes `toggleableRowNode` down to `buildBevyIconButtonSurfaceNode` on the 30 px `EVE_BUTTON_SKIN_TOKENS.bevyButton` surface, and both consumers — the Copie list (`bevy_panel_selectable_list.js`) and the Contact list (`bevy_panel_contact_view.js`) — go through it. No button material, radius, tone, pressed translation or disabled opacity is re-declared in a panel.
- What did not follow the framework was the placement: each consumer wrote `position: [0, 0]`, which glued the case to the top-left corner of its row. Both ad-hoc writes are removed; a panel never places a control with a pixel of its own.
- `eVe/elements/skin/panel_skin.js` owns the rule as `listRow.selectionInsetPx` (`scalePx(3)`, i.e. the same three pixels of air the footer close disc uses), and `bevy_panel_choice.js` exports `selectionRailPosition(containerHeightPx, sizePx)` — x = inset, y = centered — as the single owner of a rail's position.
- `toggleableRowNode` gains the additive option `containerHeightPx` (default `null`): a control dropped into a taller container says so instead of placing itself, and a caller that declares nothing keeps its historical rendering unchanged. `bevy_panel_selectable_list.js` passes `resolvedRowHeight` and `bevy_panel_contact_view.js` the doubled row height, so the 60 px Copie and Contact rows centre their case with 3 px from the edge; Contact's protected own-card column reserves the same placement (`contact_select_*_protected`).
- `railRanges` now carries `railTop` (the rail's own y inside its row) and `bevy_panel_list_selection.js` reads the finger in the row's coordinates (`range.top + range.railTop + pointerY`) for both `press` and `drag`. Without that term a finger at the top edge of a doubled row answered for the row above; absent `railTop` the term is 0, so every compact consumer keeps its old bands.
- Historical snapshot (retired owner; not current availability evidence): The five other consumers of `toggleableRowNode` — `eVe/intuition/dev/taxonomy_editor/taxonomy_editor_panel.js`, `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_info_view.js`, `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_home_nodes.js`, `eVe/intuition/runtime/bevy_panel/bevy_panel_comm_advanced_view.js` and `retired:eVe/intuition/tools/communication_inbox_view.js` — declare no container height and are byte-for-byte unchanged; `radioGroupNode` keeps its own `[0, index × row]` stacking.
- Tests: `tests/eve/bevy_panel_choice_contract.test.mjs` (anchored placement through `selectionRailPosition`, and the option-less control unchanged), `tests/eve/bevy_panel_paste_contract.test.mjs` and `tests/eve/bevy_panel_contact_pick_contract.test.mjs` (rail position of the doubled rows), `tests/eve/bevy_panel_contact_contract.test.mjs` (protected column placement), `tests/eve/bevy_panel_list_selection_contract.test.mjs` (`railTop` band reading). Real-runtime pixel acceptance in the Web 3001 / Tauri lane remains **To verify**; Tauri and iOS are not claimed.

2026-10-17 Launch wallpaper and Atom logo: `eVe/domains/user/last_known_store.js` (new) owns per-account "last known" IndexedDB stores (`createLastKnownStore` → `transact`/`read`/`write`/`remove`, failures answer null); `dashboard_items_snapshot.js` now uses it instead of its own IndexedDB helper. `eVe/user/background.js` keeps the wallpaper last presented to each account in `eve_background_presented` (params + the fetched file as bytes for a protected/media-atome wallpaper, ≤ 16 MB; cleared on logout) and presents it as soon as the account is known (`restorePresentedBackground`), then reuses that file instead of fetching it again; only an authoritative profile read (`applyProfilePreferences`) may resolve "no wallpaper" — partial `eve:profile-preferences-updated` publications (workspace, visual, weather…) no longer paint the bundled default. `window.__eveBackgroundResolutions` journals why the wallpaper resolved. Rendering: `bevy_image_texture_source.js#resolveImageRasterSize` never rasterizes an interface picture below its own box (arrival/spring scales) and has no iOS-only 512 px cap any more; `bevy_media_resource_runtime.js#shouldDeferIOSBootImageTextureResolution` defers only project-space images, so interface pictures (the home's Atom) start with the scene, and the Atom's own pictures (`__eve_dashboard_eve_(disc|ring|curve|dot)_`) are never queued behind card textures. Server: the SMS `approve` path (`auth_phone_link.js#approve`, `phone-link-approve`, challenge purpose `approve`) is removed (spec §7.4, no consumer left).

2026-10-17 One voice and waiting pairing requests: `atome/src/squirrel/voice/service.js` is the only speech path (local ONNX `fr_FR-siwis-medium` by default, `engine: 'openai'` on request; `createVoiceService({ localTts })` injectable for probes); `service_tts_runtime.js` (system speech synthesis) deleted. Server `auth_pairing.js#pending` (`pair-pending`), client `AdoleAPI.auth.listPendingPairRequests`, surfaced by `bevy_panel_home_runtime.js` (mount) and `main_menu_auth_runtime.js` (foreground, session check).
